### 2026-10-23 14:09:16  #市場

【新興市場股市受AI拋售潮與油價拖累繼續下挫】新興市場股市連續第四周收跌，投資者持續撤離AI相關持倉；油價上漲加劇通脹擔憂，進一步打壓風險偏好。明晟新興市場指數一度下跌0.8%，至1562.47點，創三個月新低，延續週四2.5%的跌幅。該指數即將迎來七個月以來持續最久的周線連跌。馬來亞銀行外匯策略師Shaun Lim表示，當前風險資產主要受兩大因素影響：AI板塊情緒轉弱以及油價上行。兩者走勢未必同步，但疊加效應會放大市場波動。週五布倫特原油交投於每桶114美元附近，本週漲幅接近4%。

### 2026-10-23 14:08:30  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Retail Sales MoM #Importance 2

UK Retail Sales Fall More Than Expected — UK retail sales volumes fell 0.4% month-on-month in September 2026, more than the expected 0.1% decline, following an upwardly revised 0.7% rise in August. The drop reflected weaker demand for autumn and winter clothing ranges amid unseasonably warm weather, alongside lower footfall and consumer cutbacks that weighed on non-food retailers. Online clothing sales weakened, while supermarket sales declined after increasing in July and August. Non-food store sales volumes dropped 0.4%, with clothing and footwear, sports equipment and household goods retailers recording declines. In contrast, automotive fuel sales rose despite higher prices. Over the third quarter, retail sales volumes increased 0.7% from the previous quarter and 1.5% year-on-year, supported by higher fuel and food store sales, warm weather, and stronger sales of alcohol and beverages linked to promotions and the World Cup. Annually, retail sales volumes rose 0.8% in September.

### 2026-10-23 13:59:14  #觀點 #國際

【萬聯證券酈彬：A股情緒集中釋放後定價將回歸基本面】萬聯證券研究所所長酈彬認為，當前A股市場情緒集中釋放，隨著“9·29”一攬子宏觀新政及近期金融街論壇系列增量政策逐步顯效，第四季度消費與投資企穩回升動能堅實，疊加三季報結構性業績亮點頻現、市場估值處於高價效比區間、中長期資金加速入市，市場信心有望迎來實質性修復，可以把握歲末年初配置視窗的時機。酈彬表示，客觀審視近期全球市場波動可以發現，儘管近期中美股市表面上呈現同頻調整，但其背後的調整邏輯存在本質差異，海外風險因素與A股基本面的直接關聯度相對有限。近期A股調整，更多是在外部情緒擾動下，場內部分存量資金因連續回撥影響帶來交易行為變化，微觀交易結構階段性走弱，帶動市場情緒趨於謹慎。隨著短期情緒層面的拋壓逐步消化，市場定價將更多回歸國內紮實的基本面與政策面。

【光大證券王開：A股市場的制度建設正從應對波動走向塑造韌性和常態化賦能】光大證券首席資產配置分析師王開表示，證監會主席吳清近日在2026金融街論壇年會上發表主旨講話，釋放的積極訊號不止在於短期提振信心，更在於回答了一個根本的問題——市場穩定靠什麼。短期靠穩市機制防風險，中期靠長線資金和機構化程序增韌性，長期靠投融資協調改革、上市公司提質增效和高水平開放提質量。把這三層連起來看，A股市場的制度建設正在從應對波動走向塑造韌性和常態化賦能。當政策託底、資金結構最佳化和盈利修復同向發力，市場信心就更有條件轉為內生修復，邁向長期投資決策。

### 2026-10-23 13:52:31  #其他

【受颱風影響 福建沿海56條客渡運航線停航 轉移13.41萬人】今天，受颱風“薔琵”影響，福建沿海出現8級及以上大風。根據福建省氣象臺預報，今天，閩東、閩中、閩南漁場和釣魚島海域最大風力9—11級、陣風11—13級；臺灣淺灘、閩外漁場及中北部沿海海區最大風力8—10級、陣風10—12級。福建省防汛抗旱指揮部維持防颱風三級應急響應。為了確保安全，截至23日11時，福建沿海共有56條客渡運航線、130艘客渡船停航，其中，4條兩岸“小三通”客運航線全部停航。111個水上施工作業專案全部停工。福建海事局已調派部署專業大型救助船、海巡船艇、應急拖輪等海上應急力量靠前值守，隨時做好應急搶險準備。目前，福建全省海上漁船和沿海養殖漁排上的人員已全部撤離，全省累計轉移海上、陸上危險區域群眾13.41萬人。

【日本能源白皮書：中東局勢暴露日本能源體系弱點】日本政府23日釋出的2025財年（2025年4月至2026年3月）能源白皮書指出，隨著中東局勢惡化，日本能源供需結構的脆弱性再次凸顯。 白皮書指出，長期以來，日本原油進口超九成依賴中東。今年2月底美國和以色列對伊朗發動軍事打擊後，作為全球能源運輸重要通道的霍爾木茲海峽通航受阻，原油及石油產品供應面臨不確定性，石腦油等部分石油相關產品的流通因此出現阻滯。 為保障國內能源供應，日本政府自3月以來採取了釋放石油儲備、加大從中東以外地區替代採購力度等措施。白皮書認為，這些措施幫助日本確保了原油供應。但與此同時，部分石油相關產品出現了因市場對供應前景擔憂而引發的囤積庫存情況，導致流通不暢和供應分佈不均。 白皮書認為，日本在上世紀70年代石油危機以後數十年間建立的能源危機應對機制，已難以應對新的能源供應風險。 日本政府通常在每年6月前後釋出能源白皮書，今年為納入中東局勢變化情況及相關應對措施，推遲至10月釋出。(新華社)

NZX 50 Ends Slightly Lower, Posts Weekly Loss — The NZX 50 shed 13 points, or 0.1%, to close at 13,734 on Friday, extending losses from the previous session and tracking a fall on Wall Street overnight as a tech-sector selloff deepened. Caution prevailed ahead of the RBNZ’s monetary policy decision next week, as well as the Fed’s interest rate decision. Although annual inflation slowed to 3.7% in Q3, caution persisted amid expectations that inflation would rise again following a temporary dip in fuel prices. Inflation also remained above the RBNZ’s 1–3% target range. Rising oil prices further pressured sentiment, raising concerns over inflation expectations and interest rate hikes. The materials, technology, and energy sectors mainly weighed on the index, with the biggest losses recorded by Fletcher Building (-4.8%), Summerset Group (-3.2%), Henderson Far East Income (-2.7%), EBOS Group (-1.9%), Contact Energy (-1.1%), and Infratil (-1.0%). For the week, the index edged down 0.1% after being almost flat in the previous week.

### 2026-10-23 13:28:35  #市場

【華創證券首席經濟學家張瑜：產業創新的載體正在重構A股的盈利結構與估值錨】華創證券首席經濟學家張瑜表示，資本市場信心的重建，長期需要落腳到企業盈利與產業創新上。產業創新對A股長期價值的賦能，本質上是把“成長性”切實轉化為“盈利性”，讓指數的上漲有利潤的支撐、有產業的根基。中游裝備製造業甚至更廣義的新經濟，正是這條邏輯下最清晰的載體。張瑜表示，產業創新的載體，即中游裝備製造業和更廣義的新經濟，正在重構A股的盈利結構與估值錨。產業競爭力和附加值的提升帶來利潤率和ROE的提升，進而帶來新經濟的市值佔比與指數中樞的長期抬升。制度完善為市場提供環境，產業創新為市場提供盈利，二者共振，A股長期價值的根基就會越扎越牢。

### 2026-10-23 13:21:57  #Trading Economics #Economy #Singapore #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Singapore Inflation Rate Edges Lower — Singapore’s annual inflation rate edged down to 2.2% in September 2026 from market expectations and August's more than two-year low of 2.3%. Price growth notably moderated for transport (7.4% vs 8.1% in August), clothing and footwear (2.1% vs 3.1%), and household durables and services (0.2% vs 1.2%). In contrast, inflation slightly picked up for food (2.4% vs 2.3%) and housing and utilities (1.4% vs 1.3%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.2%, slowing from 0.6% in August. Singapore’s core inflation eased to 2% in September from 2.2% in August, undershooting market expectations of 2.3% as the import-reliant economy weathered renewed global oil price pressures. The slowdown supported the Monetary Authority of Singapore’s recent decision to keep monetary policy unchanged. The central bank also lowered its core inflation forecast for this year to 1.5%-2%, from a previous projection of up to 2.5%, while maintaining its outlook for headline inflation at around 2%.

【西藏巴青7.0級地震 交通運輸部啟動公路交通突發事件三級應急響應】10月22日22時33分許，西藏那曲市巴青縣發生7.0級地震。交通運輸部高度重視，堅決貫徹落實習近平總書記重要指示精神和李強總理批示要求，迅速部署地震應對工作，於23日9時啟動公路交通突發事件三級應急響應，派出工作組趕赴現場，指導西藏自治區交通運輸部門全力做好搶險救援和公路保通保暢等工作。商財政部按照快速核撥機制，安排公路應急搶通資金予以支援。西藏自治區交通運輸廳在地震發生後即啟動二級應急響應，組織當地公路部門安排178人、機械裝置39臺，針對受影響的G317、G349、G345、G558、G562，S301、S205、S208、S209、S302、S506、S303等路段迅速開展排查，目前，已排查公路2377公里、橋樑295座、涵洞635道。經排查，G345、S301部分路段不同程度出現災損，震區公路已採取交通管制措施，救援車輛可保障通行，社會車輛暫不允許通行。

### 2026-10-23 13:10:11  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Manufacturing PMI #Importance 1

India Manufacturing Growth Slightly Eases — India's HSBC Manufacturing PMI edged down to 54.9 in October 2026 from 55.1 in the previous month, indicating continued growth in factory activity, albeit at a slower pace. Manufacturing output and new orders continued to expand at broadly unchanged rates, while new export orders increased at their fastest pace since June 2025. Manufacturers increased their stocks of purchases at the fastest rate in eight months, while finished goods inventories rose at the sharpest pace on record as firms responded to stronger new orders. However, growth in purchasing activity eased to its slowest pace since the current expansion began in July 2021. Manufacturing employment increased only slightly as firms had sufficient staffing capacity to handle workloads. On prices, manufacturing input cost inflation eased, while selling price inflation slowed to its weakest pace in almost four years.

### 2026-10-23 13:03:23  #Trading Economics #Economy #India #Composite PMI #Importance 2

India Private Sector Growth Picks Up in October — India’s HSBC Composite PMI rose to 56.7 in October 2026 from a final 55.9 in the prior month, flash data showed. It was the fastest growth in private-sector activity since June, with the services sector accelerating while factory output stayed resilient. New orders quickened, and foreign sales increased the most in six months, reflecting robust demand from the U.S., the UK, the Middle East and Bangladesh. However, employment rose at its slowest pace this year. Backlogs of work fell for the third time in four months, with the pace of depletion matching August 2021’s 6-1/2-year high and ranking among the sharpest since the global financial crisis. Price pressures continued to ease, as input cost inflation slowed for a seventh straight month to its lowest in near a year, while output price inflation hit its lowest since March 2025. Looking ahead, confidence reached its highest since May on expectations of stronger market conditions, although it remained below the average for 2026 so far.

### 2026-10-23 12:42:53  #Trading Economics #Markets #India #Stock Market #Importance 1

Sensex Falls for Fourth Straight Session — India’s BSE Sensex edged down about 0.4% to 72,970 on Friday, extending losses for a fourth consecutive session as surging crude oil prices and escalating geopolitical tensions continued to weigh on sentiment. Brent crude climbed to $113.50 a barrel amid heightened Middle East tensions and concerns over potential supply disruptions following Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia. Elevated oil prices pose risks to India’s import bill, inflation outlook, and corporate margins, keeping investors wary. Meanwhile, gains in technology stocks, following an overseas rebound after a sell-off in AI-linked shares, provided limited support. Most sectors opened in negative territory, with technology the only sector posting gains. Shares of Infosys, TCS, HCL Tech, and Wipro rose between 1.6% and 3.1%. Among notable laggards included State Bank of India (-0.6%), Cupid (-4.2%), Tech Mahindra (-0.9%), and BSE (-1.2%). For the week to date, the benchmark is on track for a 2.4% decline.

### 2026-10-23 12:32:28  #公司 #焦點

【中際旭創：預計1.6T、800G等高階光模組需求將保持快速增長】中際旭創午間釋出關於公司近期生產經營情況的公告，公司目前生產經營一切正常，供應鏈穩定，產能利用率充足，客戶訂單需求強勁。2017 年-2025 年，公司營業收入從23.57億元增長至382.40億元，年均複合增長率為41.67%；歸屬於上市公司股東的淨利潤從1.62億元增長至107.97億元，年均複合增長率69.03%。2026年上半年營業收入417.78億元，同比增長182.49%；歸屬於上市公司股東的淨利潤136.51億元，同比增長241.70%。在未來的經營發展中，公司將利用現有優勢資源持續拓展光互連解決方案，緊抓行業快速發展的契機，持續強化技術創新、快速量產、規模優勢、質量保障等核心競爭力，進一步最佳化和拓寬公司高階產品系列，保持經營業績穩健前行。

下游需求保持高景氣，公司在手訂單飽滿。海外市場方面，主要客戶訂單已下至2027年，部分重點客戶已給出2028年需求的明確指引，預計1.6T、800G等高階光模組需求將保持快速增長，NPO、XPO等下一代光互連產品需求亦有望逐步釋放。國內市場方面，AI算力基礎設施建設快速推進，400G、800G等產品需求亦有望進一步增長。

光互連技術持續迭代，行業技術壁壘有望進一步提升。公司將圍繞scale-out、scale-up、scale-across 和 scale-in 等全場景網路架構，積極開發下一代光互連解決方案，進一步加大對1.6T、3.2T及更高速率光模組的研發投入，同時積極開發NPO、XPO等下一代產品，目前已在部分產品上與重點客戶開展合作研發與送樣。依託公司在矽光、TFLN、相干等核心技術領域多年的沉澱和先發優勢，公司將持續鞏固技術領先地位，加快推進下一代產品的研發與產業化程序。

### 2026-10-23 12:30:06  #其他

【央視曝光養生鈦製品背後貓膩 你買的鈦製品可能是鍍鈦】從保溫杯、湯鍋到餐盤餐具，鈦制生活用品熱度持續走高。這些商品往往宣稱具有抑菌、保鮮、活化食材等作用，各類養生概念鋪天蓋地。但是同樣打著“鈦”標籤的產品，從一兩百元到一兩千元，價格卻相差不少，普通消費者很難分辨材質真偽與宣傳虛實。 南京工業大學材料科學與工程學院教授常輝介紹，價格便宜的鈦製品很可能是鍍鈦、覆鈦產品，通常是不鏽鋼或鋁，而非鈦合金或純鈦，與整體純鈦有本質區別。“塗層因為跟基體的結合強度可能不太一致，或出現脫落，一旦脫落之後，最大的風險就是在水杯裡面或者鍋具裡面會析出一些重金屬，對人體的健康造成一定危害。” 在宣傳中，還有的商家稱鈦杯可以改變水質酸鹼度、產生富氫水。專家表示，這些從科學上來講應該都是噱頭，沒有什麼科學依據。 專家表示，把鍍鈦產品冒充純鈦售賣、誇大養生功效，涉嫌虛假宣傳，嚴重的構成消費欺詐。直播間、商品頁宣傳不一致，電商平臺也要負相應的責任。（央視財經）

### 2026-10-23 12:18:48

【震盪市中壓艙石：紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）半日成交4.78億元居同類首位 近5年回報50.17％】10月23日，市場早間震盪調整，創業板指跌超2％，滬指跌1.20％。在此背景下，紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）逆勢上漲0.08％，報1.203元，換手率1.57％，半日成交額4.78億元，居同類標的ETF首位。中金公司表示，回顧過去幾年投資結構，從紅利、科技、科技成長啞鈴，自下而上的個股機會成為影響因素，即銀行股更容易在反直覺的承壓環境下跑贏。考慮當前高股息依然是銀行板塊上漲的核心邏輯，增量資金需求一方面來自險資的中長期配置需求，另一方面來自年底降低組合波動性的交易性資金需求，故銀行個股層面，建議關注幾條主線：1）業績與股息兼具的頭部區域行，包括江蘇、南京、渝農等；2）綜合收益波動率較低、分紅預期強的國有大行；3）股息邏輯可能外溢的優質頭部股份行，例如招行等。博時基金認為，後市A股整體維持結構性行情的機率較大，指數層面的方向性突破條件尚不充分。配置上宜以均衡框架應對震盪：紅利資產作為底倉對沖波動，科技方向側重前期調整較充分、三季報盈利兌現能力可驗證的細分領域，結合業績視窗擇機而非追漲。資源品在供給約束與地緣溢價下仍具備階段性對沖價值，但需注意油價衝高對中下游成本的傳導。整體以結構選擇而非倉位博弈應對不確定性。截止2026年10月22日，紅利低波ETF華泰柏瑞（512890）近5年回報50.17％，在1244只產品中排在41位。投資者可將該ETF作為底倉配置，無股票賬戶的投資者也可透過其場外聯接基金進行配置（A類：007466；C類：007467；I類：022678；Y類：022951）。

Palm Oil Set for 3rd Straight Weekly Gain — Malaysian palm oil futures extended gains, holding above MYR 4,800 per tonne amid strength in rival edible oils and a tightening supply outlook linked to severe El Niño conditions and Indonesia's upcoming B50 biodiesel mandate. Robust exports also supported prices, with cargo surveyors reporting a 58.5%–78.4% surge in shipments during Oct. 1–20 from the same period in Sept. Festival-related buying from India, the world's largest palm oil importer, has been a key demand driver. The Malaysian Palm Oil Council expects prices to stay above MYR 4,600 in Nov., noting that India and China may increase their reliance on palm oil amid anticipated supply constraints in sunflower and soybean oils. Elevated energy prices further supported prices by strengthening biodiesel demand prospects. However, gains were tempered by softer food-sector demand amid lingering inflation pressures. For the week, futures were on track for a third consecutive weekly rise, up nearly 1% so far.

### 2026-10-23 12:12:15  #Trading Economics #Economy #Malaysia #Leading Economic Index #Importance 1

Malaysia Leading Index Rises 0.2% in August — Malaysia's leading economic index increased 0.2% month-on-month in August 2026, following an upwardly revised 0.6% rise in July. The slowdown reflected declines in real imports of other basic precious and non-ferrous metals (-0.6% vs 0.1% in July), the number of housing units approved (-0.3% vs 0.04%), and real imports of semiconductors (-0.7% vs 0.4%). These declines were partly offset by faster growth in the expected sales value of manufacturing (0.6% vs. 0.1%) and steady growth in real money supply M1 (at 0.1%). On an annual basis, the leading index climbed 2.2%, accelerating from an upwardly revised 1.5% in July. Meanwhile, the coincident index, a gauge of current economic conditions, inched down 0.1% month-on-month, matching the pace recorded in July.

【機構：第三季度全球PC出貨量同比下降21%】10月23日，根據Counterpoint Research初步資料，2026年第三季度全球PC出貨量同比下降21%至5700萬臺，環比下降13.8%。主要由於儲存及其他零部件成本持續高企，推動PC價格上漲，並對市場需求形成壓力。2026年第四季度全球PC出貨量預計將同比下降27%，2027年全年出貨量預計與2026年基本持平。其中，聯想2026年第三季度出貨量為1500萬臺，出貨量同比下降23%，市場份額為26.3%；惠普以1030萬臺的出貨量位居第二，同比下降31%，市場份額為18.1%；戴爾以760萬臺的出貨量位居第三，同比下降25%，市場份額為13.3%；蘋果出貨量同比下降11%至560萬臺，市場份額從上年同期的8.7%提升至9.8%；華碩出貨量為520萬臺，同比下降7%。

Rupee Rally Stalls on Rising Oil Prices, Dollar Demand — The Indian rupee hovered around 94.8 per dollar, easing slightly from multi-week highs as rising oil prices and a global flight to safety weighed on the currency, while persistent dollar demand threatened to limit further gains. Brent crude climbed to nearly USD 115 a barrel amid concerns over supply disruptions following Houthi attacks on Saudi Arabia, raising India’s import costs and potentially spurring further hedging by importers. Meanwhile, the rupee’s eight-session rally ended on Thursday as the currency reversed from a more than six-week high near 94.30. Although the RBI’s recent foreign-exchange measures have curbed dollar demand and provided some relief, they have not addressed underlying pressures from elevated oil prices and capital outflows.

【一家鮮為人知的韓國船東9月份主導了霍爾木茲海峽石油運輸】市場情報機構的資料顯示，9月份，一家鮮為人知的韓國船東在霍爾木茲海峽的石油運輸中佔據了主導地位；該集團旗下船隻運輸的石油總量，超過了緊隨其後的三家船東的總和。根據市場情報機構Vortexa、Equasis和Signal Group提供的船舶追蹤及其他資料分析，韓國長錦商船集團（Sinokor Group）旗下的船隻透過這條連線波斯灣與全球市場的航道，運輸了1.056億桶原油。這一數字約佔上個月經由該航道運輸的石油總量的40%。根據機構基於上述資料進行的計算，排名其後的三家企業——沙特國家航運公司（Bahri）、科威特石油油輪公司以及阿布扎比國家石油公司（ADNOC）旗下的航運部門——在同期內的運輸總量合計為9730萬桶。該分析基於船舶所有權，而非船舶運營商。

### 2026-10-23 11:27:42  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Corn Holds Near Two-Month Low — Corn futures held around $4.6 per bushel, near their lowest level in more than two months as weak export shipments and US harvest pressure outweighed stronger new-crop sales. USDA data showed US corn export sales for the 2026/27 marketing year rose 36% week over week to 60.1 million bushels in the week ended October 15, exceeding analysts' estimates and signaling solid demand for future deliveries. However, export inspections plunged nearly 49% to 672,475 metric tons, marking the lowest weekly total since October 2024 and highlighting a slowdown in actual shipments. The US harvest reached 44% completion as of October 18, up from 33% a week earlier, with dry weather across much of the Midwest expected to help fieldwork advance. The USDA projects record US production of 16.03 billion bushels and ending stocks of 1.849 billion bushels for 2026/27, reinforcing concerns over ample availability. These supply pressures continue to limit the market's recovery despite stronger export bookings.

### 2026-10-23 11:12:11  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Soybeans Rebound on Strong Export Demand — Soybean futures rose toward $12.6 per bushel, rebounding from a two-month low reached on October 21, as strong export demand led by China helped end an eight-session losing streak. The USDA reported 2.84 million metric tons of net US soybean export sales for the 2026/27 marketing year in the week ended October 15, sharply above 574,900 tons a week earlier. China accounted for 2.18 million tons, while export shipments climbed 75% week over week to 2.02 million tons, up 88% from the four-week average. A separate USDA announcement also confirmed a sale of 202,000 tons to China. Still, futures remained on track for a second consecutive weekly loss as the advancing US harvest and expectations for a large crop continued to weigh on prices. The USDA projects US soybean production at 4.56 billion bushels for the 2026/27 marketing year. However, analysts suggested harvest-related pressure was easing as fieldwork neared completion.

Indonesia Money Supply Growth Edges Up — Indonesia’s broad money supply (M2) rose 8.3% year-on-year to a record IDR 10,587.9 trillion in September 2026, edging up from 8.2% in the previous month. The increase was mainly driven by narrow money supply (M1), which grew 9%, while quasi-money growth accelerated to 7.3%. By component, net claims on the central government (Pempus) rebounded 24.4% after declining 7% in August, while net foreign assets returned to growth of 1% following a 6.6% contraction. However, credit growth eased to 12.7% in September from 13.3% in the previous month.

US Natgas Prices Fall Back to 2-Month Low — US natural gas prices fell back to $2.76 per MMBtu on Friday, their lowest level in two months, as mild weather forecasts weighed on expected heating demand. Above-average temperatures are expected across the Midwest and East through November 5, according to Commodity Weather Group, likely limiting gas consumption. This outweighed support from a smaller-than-usual storage build, strong LNG feedgas demand, and lower production. EIA data showed that energy companies added 77 bcf of natural gas to storage in the week ended October 16, below the 86 bcf injected during the same week last year and the five-year average of 87 bcf. Meanwhile, average gas flows to the nine major US LNG export facilities rose to 18.2 bcfd in October, up from 17.9 bcfd in September. On the supply side, natural gas production across the Lower 48 states averaged 111.4 bcfd this month, down from record highs of 113.3 bcfd in August and September.

US 10-Year Yield Holds at One-Month Low — The yield on the US 10-year Treasury note held around 4.97% on Friday after falling by more than 10 basis points in the previous session to its lowest level in a month. The decline came as a deepening selloff in technology and artificial intelligence stocks prompted investors to seek safer assets, including government bonds. Growing concerns over the sustainability of AI investments, a key driver of US economic growth, also prompted markets to scale back expectations for further Federal Reserve rate hikes. Markets are now pricing in roughly a 91% probability that the Fed will leave interest rates unchanged next week, while the likelihood of a rate increase in December stands at around 80%. Meanwhile, escalating tensions in the Middle East, amid reports that the US and Israel are preparing potential renewed strikes against Iran after the midterm elections, further strengthened demand for safe-haven assets.

Indonesian Stocks Rise for 4th Session, Set for Solid Weekly Gain — Indonesia’s IDX Composite rose 40 points, or 0.7%, to 5,422 in early trading on Friday, up for the fourth session, amid gains in energy, cyclical, industrial, and infrastructure stocks. Sentiment was supported by exchange reforms that will exclude stocks classified as having High Shareholding Concentration from composite index calculations starting Nov. 2. The move follows MSCI warnings that Indonesian equities risk being downgraded from emerging to standalone market status. Meanwhile, several listed companies, including major banks, have launched share buyback programs to restore investor confidence and support sliding valuations. However, gains were capped by fiscal concerns as oil prices hovered around USD 100 per barrel, while accelerating food prices added to inflationary pressures. Bukit Asam rose 3.5%, along with Astra Intl. (2.9%), and XLSmart Telecom (2.5%). For the week, the index was on track to rise around 4%, partially recovering from a nearly 15% plunge in the prior week.

Philippines 10-Year Yield Hits 8-Year High — The Philippines’ 10-year government bond yield climbed to 8.10% in late October, its highest level since October 2018, following the Bangko Sentral ng Pilipinas’ (BSP) larger-than-expected rate hike. The central bank raised its key policy rate by 50 bps to 5.50%, exceeding expectations for a 25 bps increase and marking its fourth consecutive hike this year. The decision came as annual inflation accelerated to 7.2% in September from 6.1% in August, overshooting forecasts of 6.6% and matching April’s three-year high. Higher global oil prices amid the ongoing Middle East tensions and potential El Niño-related supply disruptions threaten to sustain inflationary pressures, while peso weakness raises the cost of imported goods. Meanwhile, subdued demand at recent government bond auctions added to upward pressure on yields, as investors demanded higher returns to compensate for persistent inflation risks.

Japan’s 10-Year Yield Drops on Haven Demand — Japan’s 10-year government bond yield fell by more than 10 basis points to around 2.76% on Friday, reaching its lowest level since early August as a global selloff in technology and artificial intelligence stocks drove investors toward safer assets. During periods of heightened market uncertainty, Japanese investors often repatriate overseas investments, benefiting the yen and Japanese government bonds. Meanwhile, data showed that both headline and core inflation in Japan rose 2.1% year-on-year in September, marking their highest levels in nine months as yen weakness and elevated energy prices increased import costs. With underlying inflation returning above the Bank of Japan’s 2% target, market expectations for another interest rate hike this year have strengthened. The BOJ is widely expected to maintain its current policy rate at its upcoming meeting, with a potential increase in December.

Asian equities fell alongside Wall Street. Japan 10-year bond prices jumped, sending yields down about 10bp; US Treasuries largely held Thursday’s gains. Commonwealth Bank strategist Carol Kong said investors prefer the yen to the dollar as a safe haven after US mega-cap earnings dented the AI-driven growth narrative and added that further tech de-risking could push the yen higher.

China Stocks Slide to Over 2-Month Low — The Shanghai Composite lost 1.5% to 3,602 on Friday, while the Shenzhen Component slid 2.1% to 11,118, with both indexes hitting their lowest levels in more than two months as steep losses in semiconductor and technology stocks weighed on the market. The selloff mirrored broader declines across Asian markets following overnight losses on Wall Street, as mounting concerns over the sustainability and potential returns of artificial intelligence investments pressured technology stocks. Domestically, MiniMax Group Inc. and Z.AI Co. have lost a combined $150 billion in market value from their respective peaks amid intensifying competition and the prospect of rival listings. Potential IPOs by DeepSeek and Moonshot AI could further divert investor capital from MiniMax and Zhipu, two of China’s few listed pure-play AI firms. Notable laggards included Cambricon Technologies (-3.7%), SMIC (-2.9%), Zhongji Innolight (-3.6%), Eoptolink Technology (-6.1%) and NAURA Technology (-1.9%).

### 2026-10-23 10:06:02  #MKT News #Market Regions #Greater China #CSI300 #Impact bullish #SSE Composite #CNH

【Guan Tao: Take a rational view of overseas market turbulence spillovers】Guan Tao, chief economist at Huafu Securities, said recent domestic market weakness reflects sentiment volatility after rising geopolitical tensions and widening divergence in overseas tech stocks, but key market drivers remain intact. He listed four market-positive points: China’s long-term favorable economic fundamentals are unchanged; the certainty of stronger, more effective policy support remains; innovation momentum is gradually releasing; and valuation advantages in Chinese assets remain to be exploited. Under dual forces of market demand and policy guidance, China’s growth is shifting toward higher quality and structural upgrade. Investors should treat recent volatility rationally, focus on earnings-recovery stories, and remain cautiously optimistic on medium-to-long-term market prospects. Guan added that deepening capital-market financing and investment reform is two-way: authorities must strengthen institutional support for long-term capital and investors must adopt long-term, value-driven, rational investment habits. The current episode is a litmus test for the market’s ability to move away from momentum trading toward greater maturity.

【六部門出臺意見 推進老舊街區廠區改造提升】從住房城鄉建設部獲悉，住房城鄉建設部、工業和資訊化部等六部門近日聯合印發《關於推進老舊街區廠區改造提升的指導意見》，明確了各類老舊街區廠區的改造提升重點。按照要求，各地要結合城市體檢結果，將房屋和市政基礎設施老化、公共服務和公共空間短缺、功能產業和發展活力有待提升的街區廠區作為改造提升物件，統籌確定改造提升範圍和實施時序。同時，制定老舊街區廠區改造提升專案實施方案，將城市設計要求納入專案實施方案，引導街區廠區高質量改造提升。其中，重點要做好五項工作：推動老舊廠區功能轉換推動老舊商業街區業態升級推動老舊生活街區品質提升推動老舊交通樞紐及周邊街區融合發展加強歷史文化街區鎮區保護利用為做好相關工作，各地要健全全生命週期實施機制，發揮政府引導作用，加大政策支援力度。同時，建立可持續的投融資模式，支援各類企業參與老舊街區廠區改造提升，規範市場化運作流程等。

### 2026-10-23 10:05:57  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Platinum Sinks to 11-Month Low — Platinum futures dropped below $1,560 an ounce, hitting their lowest level since November 2025, as a liquidity-driven selloff swept across precious metals amid a broader stock market rout. Growing concerns over the sustainability of AI investment triggered a sharp decline in US stocks and a rally in Treasuries, prompting investors to liquidate gold and other liquid assets to meet margin calls and cover losses elsewhere. Non-yielding metals also remained under pressure from rising oil prices, which heightened inflation concerns and strengthened expectations that interest rates could stay elevated for longer. Platinum also faces fundamental headwinds, with the World Platinum Investment Council forecasting a 265,000-ounce market surplus in 2026, as weaker investment demand and softer jewelry consumption weigh on the outlook. Automotive demand is also expected to decline 4%, partly offset by a projected 5% increase in industrial demand.

【管濤：理性看待海外資本市場動盪對國內的溢位影響】受地緣衝突緊張局勢升級、海外科技股行情演進分歧加大的影響，近期國內資本市場調整加劇，反映了市場情緒的波動。華福證券首席經濟學家管濤在接受採訪時表示，從以下方面看，中國資產的配置價值更加凸顯，資本市場走勢與之存在一定錯位。一是經濟長期向好的基本面沒有變；二是政策加力提效的確定性沒有變；三是創新動能正在逐步釋放；四是市場估值優勢有待挖掘。管濤表示，在市場需求和政策引導的雙重驅動下，中國經濟向新向優、結構升級趨勢明確，投資者應理性看待市場震盪，把握業績修復主線，對市場中長期走勢保持審慎樂觀。同時，深化資本市場投融資綜合改革是“雙向奔赴”，既要從制度上不斷完善長錢長投的資本市場生態環境，也要投資者不斷強化長期投資、價值投資、理性投資的投資理念。當前正是前述投資理念的“試金石”，是中國資本市場擺脫“追漲殺跌”、走向更加成熟的又一次大考。（證券時報）

### 2026-10-23 10:01:37  #Trading Economics #Markets #New Zealand #Currency #Importance 1

New Zealand Dollar Hovers Near 1-Month High — The New Zealand dollar edged up to $0.569, trading near a one-month high, as expectations of further monetary tightening by the Reserve Bank supported the currency. Although inflation eased slightly more than expected in the third quarter, analysts expect price pressures to pick up again, potentially pushing inflation well above 4% in the fourth quarter and keeping it outside the RBNZ’s 1-3% target range for longer than previously anticipated. This outlook was reinforced by rising energy prices amid heightened tensions in the Middle East. Markets are almost certain that the central bank will deliver a third rate hike next week, bringing the OCR to 3.0%. Beyond that, some economists project the OCR to climb to 3.75% by the first quarter of next year or peak at 4% in the second half of 2027. The kiwi has gained 0.7% this week, marking its second consecutive weekly advance.

【China Association of Automobile Manufacturers' Fu Bingfeng: Firms must prioritize breakthroughs in key core technologies】China Association of Automobile Manufacturers vice chairman and secretary-general Fu Bingfeng said at the 2026 China Auto Supply Chain Conference that shifting geopolitics and regional trade protectionism have severely disrupted auto supply-chain stability. He said that at the start of the '15th Five-Year' period maintaining volume and improving quality is the development theme and urged industry and firms to prioritize breakthroughs in key core technologies—vehicle-grade chips, in-vehicle operating systems, solid-state batteries, new electronic/electrical architectures and basic industrial software—to shore up supply-chain security. He also called for deeper integration of electrification, intelligence and green development, stronger OEM-supplier coordination and cross-sector cooperation, and co-innovation between vehicle makers and domestic and foreign core suppliers to boost industry resilience and innovation efficiency.

【國泰海通於博：人民幣溫和升值不會影響出口韌性】年初以來，雖然人民幣兌美元匯率整體走強，但出口仍然保持較高景氣。對此，國泰海通首席宏觀分析師於博表示，人民幣兌美元升值不等於對所有貿易伙伴貨幣同步升值，國內弱通脹也在緩衝實際升值壓力。出口向技術含量更高、替代性更低的商品轉移，疊加進口中間品形成的自然對沖，削弱了出口對匯率的敏感性。人民幣結算與外匯套保日益普及，降低了匯率波動向企業經營的傳導。人民幣溫和升值不會影響出口韌性，歷史上的匯率與市場份額同升，更多源於產業競爭力提高。

Australia 10Y Yield Falls to 7-Week Low — Australia’s 10-year government bond yield fell below 5.2%, hitting a seven-week low as a tech-led global equity selloff sparked demand for safe-haven assets. Concerns over the enormous capital requirements of AI development and the sustainability of heavy data centre investment fueled fears that a slowdown in spending could weigh on economic growth. The concerns triggered a rally in US Treasuries, spilling over into the Australian bond market. The move came despite oil prices surpassing $113 per barrel, threatening renewed inflation pressures. RBA Deputy Governor Andrew Hauser warned that the inflation outlook remained materially worse due partly to elevated oil prices, stressing the need to anchor inflation expectations ahead of the November 3 meeting, where policymakers will consider a fifth rate hike this year. Markets currently imply a 64% chance of a quarter-point rise in November. Focus now turns to Q3 inflation numbers due next week for further clues on the policy outlook.

Australian Stocks Set to Post Modest Weekly Drop — Australian shares fell 28 points or 0.3% to 8,763 around noon on Friday, extending prior losses amid weaker U.S. stock futures after tech names on Wall Street sold off overnight on concerns that the AI trade may be faltering. Locally, fears of a fifth interest rate hike this year persisted after Reserve Bank Deputy Gov. Hauser said the inflation outlook "looks materially worse" than previous estimates. Meanwhile, flash October PMI was subdued, with both manufacturing and services sectors continuing to drop. Tech, non-energy minerals, and industrial services dragged the benchmark index, partly offset by strength in energy minerals and communications. BHP Group tumbled 3.1% due to lower copper output and softening iron ore prices. Gold stocks also slipped, dragged by Northern Star Resources (-2.5%) and Evolution Mining (-3.1%). In contrast, three of four big banks rallied, all up above 2%. For the week, markets are heading for the first weekly fall in four, down around 0.5% so far.

【Analyst: Japan's 2.4% underlying CPI raises odds of a higher terminal rate】Eamonn Sheridan says Japan's September underlying inflation accelerated. Despite government fuel subsidies, goods and services price gains widened. Core CPI ex‑fresh food but including fuel rose 2.1% YoY (in line); core CPI ex‑fresh food and fuel — the BOJ's preferred gauge — climbed from 1.9% to 2.4% YoY. Goods inflation rose to 2.8% from 2.6% and services inflation to 1.5% from 1.2%, signalling firms are passing higher wages to customers. Rising fuel costs tied to the prolonged Middle East conflict, yen weakness lifting import prices, and robust AI‑related demand have bluntly undermined the subsidy effect. The print reinforces market bets on a BOJ hike in December and shifts attention to how high policy rates could go; BOJ wording in next week's Outlook Report may shape expectations for rates after December as it balances broader inflation against signs of cooling labour market and domestic demand.

【Japan weighs phasing out gasoline subsidies from December as costs climb】Japan is considering cutting gasoline subsidies as early as December, two ruling-party officials said, citing rapidly rising program costs. The emergency measure, introduced in March to blunt fuel-price inflation and keep average regular gasoline around 170 yen per liter (≈$1.09), now costs tens of billions of dollars a month and is quickly depleting a 3.1 trillion yen supplementary budget approved in June. Under a plan being discussed, weekly payments to fuel distributors would be phased down, lifting the national average retail price to about 175 yen/l in December and roughly 180 yen/l in January.

### 2026-10-23 09:23:05  #宏觀 #焦點

【商務部消費促進司負責人談2026年前三季度我國消費市場情況】前三季度，商務部認真貫徹落實黨中央、國務院決策部署，深入實施提振消費專項行動，出臺一系列促消費政策措施，推動消費市場規模穩步擴大、居民消費結構持續最佳化，為經濟增長髮揮重要作用。一、消費“主引擎”作用持續鞏固，超大規模市場優勢日益凸顯。前三季度，最終消費支出對經濟增長的貢獻率為46.7%，高於上半年0.7個百分點，拉動GDP增長2.1個百分點，持續保持經濟增長第一拉動力。二、“商品+服務”消費並重發展，保持良好增長勢頭。前三季度，社會消費商品和服務零售總額同比（下同）增長2.6%，增速比1-8月快0.1個百分點。其中，服務零售額增長5.0%，增速高於商品零售額4個百分點。全國居民人均消費支出2.2萬元，比上年同期名義增長3.6%，扣除價格因素，實際增長2.7%。三、社會消費品零售總額有所回升，商品消費基本面良好。9月份社會消費品零售總額增長1.1%，增速比8月份快0.7個百分點，除汽車以外消費品零售額增長3.9%。前三季度，社會消費品零售總額增長1.1%，在去年同期高基數情況下實現平穩增長。限額以上單位16大類商品中，9類商品保持增長，其中6類商品增速在5%以上。四、綠色、智慧、健康消費需求旺盛，向新向好特徵明顯。部分升級類商品較快增長，限額以上單位通訊器材、化妝品零售額分別增長18.1%、6.1%。商務部商務大資料顯示，前三季度重點平臺智慧眼鏡銷售額增長1.1倍、外骨骼助力器銷售額增長46.4%、具身智慧機器人銷售額增長22.6%、有機食品銷售額增長6.4%。據汽車流通協會資料，9月份新能源乘用車零售滲透率達67.0%。（商務部）

### 2026-10-23 09:22:32  #宏觀

【應急管理部將國家地震災害應急響應提升至三級】記者23日從應急管理部獲悉，針對西藏那曲市巴青縣地震，國務院抗震救災指揮部辦公室、應急管理部將國家地震災害應急響應由四級提升至三級，派出工作組赴震區指導協助。10月22日22時33分，西藏那曲市巴青縣發生7.0級地震，已造成16人遇難，54人受傷。地震發生後，國務院抗震救災指揮部副總指揮、應急管理部有關負責人持續排程指導應急處置工作。應急管理部指導西藏自治區應急管理廳調派翼龍無人機趕赴災區，開展災情偵察任務。據瞭解，國家綜合性消防救援隊伍共調派350人、83車出動救援，截至23日6時30分，已有120人、27車抵達災區，西藏消防救援總隊230人、56車在增援途中，均已行進至那曲境內。（新華社）

【青花郎、古井貢古20重挫 普五四連陰跌至789元 飛天茅臺零售均價小幅上漲】新浪財經“酒價內參”過去24小時收集的資料顯示，10月23日中國白酒業12大單品的終端零售均價兩漲七跌三平，下跌品種繼續佔據多數，且跌幅明顯大於上漲端，整體價盤連續第三日下行。上漲端，飛天茅臺小幅上漲1元至1789元，結束此前四連陰，低位小幅修復；國窖1573上漲1元至888元，昨日平盤後微幅回升，短線跌勢暫時緩和。下跌端，精品茅臺下跌2元至2458元，三連陰持續調整，繼續向下尋底；五糧液普五八代下跌5元至789元，四連陰累計下挫18元，跌破800元后弱勢進一步加深；五糧液1618下跌1元至829元，連續第二日小幅回落；青花汾20下跌3元至386元，三連陰持續探底，近三日從400元關口一路下滑；洋河夢之藍M6+下跌2元至605元，連續兩日回落，跌破近期趨勢線；古井貢古20大跌9元至518元，連續第三日大幅走弱，前期反彈漲幅全部回吐還有虧空；青花郎再跌9元至690元，700元關口失守後跌勢繼續擴大，淪為今日跌幅最大單品之一。持平方面，茅臺1935維持684元不變，連續第四日平盤；習酒君品保持632元，兩連跌後轉入橫盤整理；水晶劍南春維持409元不變，連續回落後暫時企穩。

今日12大單品的終端零售總價繼續下探，七款產品價格下跌，其中古井貢古20、青花郎和五糧液普五八代的跌幅較為突出，上漲端僅飛天茅臺和國窖1573小幅回升，未能扭轉整體弱勢。如果12大單品各取一瓶整體打包售賣，今日總售價為10677元，較昨日再跌29元，連續第三日下行，並創下近37天以來最低紀錄。

Asian Stocks Track Wall Street Lower — Asian stocks fell sharply on Friday, mirroring overnight losses on Wall Street as growing concerns over the sustainability of artificial intelligence investments triggered a broad selloff in technology shares. Semiconductor makers, memory chip producers and other AI-related companies recorded significant declines, while investors moved toward safer assets such as government bonds and the yen. Sentiment was further weighed down by escalating tensions in the Middle East, following reports that the US and Israel had drawn up plans for possible renewed strikes against Iran after next month’s midterm elections. Technology-heavy markets in Japan and South Korea led regional losses, with the Nikkei 225 and KOSPI Composite each falling nearly 2%.

### 2026-10-23 09:03:45  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Currency #Importance 2

Yen Rallies on Haven Buying, Strong Inflation — The Japanese yen strengthened toward 156 per dollar on Friday, reaching its highest level in five weeks as a global selloff in technology and artificial intelligence stocks boosted demand for safe-haven assets. The equity downturn intensified this week after Meta Platforms’ plans to increase capital expenditure reignited concerns that technology companies may take on more debt to fund the AI buildout. In Japan, data showed that both headline and core inflation rose 2.1% year-on-year in September, marking their highest levels in nine months as yen weakness and elevated energy prices drove up import costs. With underlying inflation returning above the Bank of Japan’s 2% target, market expectations for another interest rate hike this year have strengthened. The BOJ is widely anticipated to leave rates unchanged at its upcoming meeting, potentially paving the way for a rate increase in December.

Singapore Home Price Growth Confirmed at Nearly 2-Year High — Private home prices in Singapore rose by 1.4% quarter-on-quarter in the third quarter of 2026, in line with initial estimates and marking the fastest growth since the fourth quarter of 2024. The latest result accelerated from a 0.5% increase in the previous quarter, as landed property prices climbed 2.9%, up from 2.5% in Q2, while non-landed prices rebounded 1.0% after falling 0.1%. By region, prices in the Outside Central Region rose 2.3%, recovering from a 0.1% decline, while gains in the Core Central Region moderated to 0.3% from 1.8%. Prices in the Rest of Central Region were unchanged after falling 1.2% in the previous quarter. Meanwhile, private residential rents increased 1.3%, accelerating from 0.7% in Q2, supported by stronger rental growth in the Outside Central Region. The government will maintain a high level of housing supply, with 9,320 private residential units on the 2026 Confirmed List, more than 50% above the 10-year annual average.

【AI worries fuel risk-off; 10-year JGB yield drops 9bps to 2.79%】Japanese government bond yields fell across the curve on Friday as AI-related equity selling boosted safe-haven demand and US Treasury gains spilled into JGBs. The 10-year JGB yield slid about 9 bps to 2.79%; the 20-year fell 5.5 bps. The move came despite a key Japanese inflation gauge rising above the Bank of Japan’s 2% target this year for the first time, a data point that had supported market bets on a December BOJ rate hike. Market participants said focus shifted to growth risk and AI-driven volatility, temporarily reducing the market’s emphasis on inflation; long-duration bonds attracted demand for diversification and carry.

### 2026-10-23 08:53:57  #市場 #央行 #國際

【中信期貨：能源價格上漲強化美聯儲緊縮預期，黃金短期維持震盪偏弱判斷】中東局勢升級使能源供應風險重新成為市場關注焦點，油價上漲對黃金的影響開始更多體現為貨幣政策約束。海灣能源設施遇襲及霍爾木茲海峽通航風險升溫，推升原油供應風險溢價，也使美國通脹回落面臨新的不確定性。在此前通脹已表現出較強黏性的背景下，能源價格持續上漲可能進一步延長美聯儲維持限制性政策的時間，壓制市場對未來寬鬆空間的預期。美聯儲政策定價正在從10月按兵不動轉向關注12月是否進一步加息。美聯儲主席近期強調政策將逐次會議評估，未對後續利率路徑作出明確承諾，紐約聯儲官員亦釋放年內仍可能加息的訊號。當前10月維持利率不變仍是市場基準情景，但油價上漲疊加通脹資料偏強，使12月進一步收緊的預期有所強化。對黃金而言，短期關鍵在於市場是否繼續上修終端利率預期，以及美債收益率能否隨之回落。地緣衝突對黃金的影響存在兩條相反的傳導路徑。一方面，衝突升級增加避險需求；另一方面，能源供應衝擊推升通脹風險，並透過美聯儲政策預期抬高黃金持有成本。近期金價承壓表明，市場對利率路徑的重新定價暫時壓過避險買盤，後續需重點觀察油價上漲是否進一步傳導至通脹預期和實際利率。中長期看，黃金配置需求仍具韌性。中國央行延續增持黃金，海外黃金ETF保持資金淨流入，反映官方儲備多元化及機構資產配置需求尚未逆轉。但在短期貨幣政策預期重新收緊的環境下，配置資金更多體現為下方支撐，難以立即推動金價重回上行趨勢。（中信期貨）

【Japan manufacturing expansion slows to seven-month low】Japan's manufacturing expansion slowed to a seven-month low in October as output and new orders growth eased, S&P Global's preliminary PMI showed. The manufacturing PMI fell to 53.3 in October from 54.1 in September; manufacturers' new orders and output growth were the weakest since March, and private-sector new export growth slowed to a four-month low. Manufacturing employment rose modestly, while purchasing increased and inventories rose at the fastest pace in nearly four years. S&P Global Japan services PMI business activity index rose to 52.0 from 51.3, with activity and sales gains accelerating. S&P Global Market Intelligence economist Usama Bhatty said: "The preliminary October PMIs show overall Japanese business activity growth was flat month-on-month and remained below the year-to-date average."

Japanese Shares Follow Wall Street Lower — The Nikkei 225 Index dropped 1.6% to around 61,900 on Friday, hitting its lowest levels in nearly three months and tracking a tech-led selloff on Wall Street overnight amid mounting concerns about the artificial intelligence trade. Semiconductor makers, memory chip producers and other AI-linked stocks suffered heavy losses, while investors shifted toward safer assets, including government bonds and the yen. Market sentiment was further dampened by rising tensions in the Middle East, following reports that the US and Israel had prepared plans for potential renewed strikes against Iran after next month’s midterm elections. Major technology stocks led the decline, with Kioxia falling 7.6%, Advantest dropping 4.2%, SoftBank Group losing 7.5%, Tokyo Electron sliding 6% and Fujikura retreating 6.8%. Japan Manufacturing Growth Eases to 7-Month Low — The S&P Global Japan Manufacturing PMI declined to 53.3 in October 2026 from 54.1 in September, falling below market forecasts of 54.5, preliminary estimates showed. It was the slowest expansion in the manufacturing sector since March, as firms recorded slower increases in output and new orders, with both growing at their weakest rates in seven months. Employment continued to increase, with job creation rising moderately. Purchasing activity also increased, contributing to the largest accumulation of stocks of purchases in almost four years. Meanwhile, suppliers’ delivery times lengthened substantially. On the price front, input and output costs continued to rise. Looking ahead, business sentiment remained positive.

Japan Composite PMI Stays at 4-Month Low — Japan’s S&P Global Composite PMI Business Activity Index held steady at 52.3 in October 2026, unchanged from September’s final reading, flash data showed. This marked the 19th consecutive month of private-sector expansion, although growth remained at its slowest pace since May. Stronger services activity was accompanied by the slowest increase in manufacturing output since March. New orders grew at their weakest pace in three months, while new export orders continued to rise. Employment growth slowed to a 14-month low, with hiring trends diverging between sectors, while backlogs of work increased further. On the price front, input costs rose sharply, but selling price inflation eased to a four-month low. Meanwhile, persistent inflation concerns weighed on business sentiment, with year-ahead output expectations falling to their lowest level since July.

US Futures Stabilize After Tech-Led Rout — US stock futures were little changed on Friday following a sharp decline in technology shares during the previous session, as growing concerns over the sustainability of artificial intelligence investments weighed on sentiment. During Thursday’s regular session, the Nasdaq Composite and S&P 500 fell 2.6% and 1.21%, respectively, while the Dow edged up 0.02%. Major laggards included memory chipmakers Micron (-6.3%) and SanDisk (-8.5%), semiconductor giants Nvidia (-4.4%) and AMD (-8.1%), as well as Elon Musk’s companies SpaceX (-9.1%) and Tesla (-3.1%). The selloff followed Meta Platforms’ (-7%) plans to increase capital expenditure, reigniting concerns that technology companies may take on additional debt to fund their AI infrastructure expansion. Meanwhile, escalating tensions in the Middle East added further pressure on equities, prompting investors to seek the relative safety of bonds.

【RBNZ faces inflation rebound; economists expect Oct. 28 rate hike】Economists say the RBNZ will need to hike sharply to rein in a rebound in inflation, even if that requires action 10 days before the election. Q3 CPI eased slightly, but most analysts expect Q4 inflation to accelerate above 4% and remain above the RBNZ’s 1–3% target for longer than the bank had projected. Rising fuel prices and a weaker NZD are the main upside pressures. Most economists expect the official cash rate to be raised to 3% next Wednesday (Oct. 28). ASB senior economist Smith warns sustained higher inflation is a major problem, forecasts CPI above 3% until Q4 2027, and expects the RBNZ on Oct. 28 to acknowledge more work is needed and to retain a conditional bias to tighten further.

### 2026-10-23 07:55:42  #MKT News #Important

【Analysts: Fed repricing, deleveraging push gold and silver lower】Precious metals fell broadly. Short-term moves in the USD index and U.S. Treasury yields will dominate price direction; the October Fed meeting and international oil will be the main upstream drivers. Huishang Futures says the pullback is driven by rising Fed rate-hike expectations and concentrated long liquidations: stronger U.S. jobs and inflation data have repriced a higher-for-longer policy path, lifting real yields and the dollar and raising the carrying cost of non-yielding metals. Fading geopolitical risk premia and weaker industrial demand have amplified silver’s decline. Jinrui Futures attributes the rout to a reversal in the macro rate backdrop and trade-driven deleveraging: deteriorating financial conditions set the common downwards direction, while differences in market liquidity, investor structure and industrial demand explain cross-commodity dispersion. After the prior sharp correction, a technical rebound is possible, but any sustained recovery depends on the subsequent paths of U.S. yields and the USD.

【Japan September core inflation above Bank of Japan target, bolstering odds of December rate hike】Japan's September core CPI excluding fresh food rose 2.1% YoY, up from 1.7% in August, a print that could reinforce market expectations of Bank of Japan tightening in December. Markets are pricing a potential 25bps move that would lift the policy rate to about 1.5%, though the BOJ is widely expected to leave rates unchanged at next week's meeting to assess the impact of prior hikes, including September's. A weaker yen and higher international oil prices are raising import costs and keeping upward pressure on consumer prices. With underlying inflation now close to the BOJ's 2% target, Governor UEDA said the bank must pay greater attention to upside inflation risks.

【UK to Rely More on Gas Imports This Winter; European LNG Competition Could Push Prices Higher】Britain is expected to rely more on gas imports this winter. Despite a recent rise in domestic supply, global market tightness leaves the UK exposed to price spikes. The national gas transmission operator said North Sea output is forecast to fall about 5% in the current heating season; North Sea supplies account for roughly one-third of UK winter gas demand. LNG imports are expected to rise about 5% to cover the shortfall, while Norwegian pipeline flows are forecast to increase about 1%. The operator said even under extreme stress scenarios the UK would have "adequate gas supply capacity", but warned that "strong European LNG demand could intensify competition for cargoes and push up prices." UK gas futures have nearly tripled year-to-date, trading at the highest levels since 2022.

【中小銀行債券投資“攻守換防” 久期策略迴歸審慎】近日，10年期國債收益率持續下行，截至10月22日，已觸及歷史低位1.6414%。機構對長端品種交易態度趨於謹慎。“我們對金融投資規模的控制早已開始，今年以來未新增債券持倉。”西部地區某農商行金融市場部人士坦言。不過，也有中小銀行稱，債券投資業務仍按原有節奏開展。中小銀行債券投資的底層邏輯正在重塑。過去兩年，不少中小銀行依賴拉長久期、透過交易盤博取波段價差來支撐非息收入。如今，這一模式遭遇三重壓力：監管引導、風控顧慮，以及負債端約束。在此背景下，中小銀行的操作從“賣短買長”切換為“買短賣長”，整體策略由交易主導逐步轉向以票息鎖定和流動性管理為核心的被動配置。（中國證券報）

### 2026-10-23 07:16:31  #市場

【私募策略分化 CTA策略今年以來平均收益近9%】近期，隨著槓桿資金主動降風險，前期擁擠的人工智慧（AI）硬體賽道持續呈現資金流出，機構配置再平衡的程序正在進一步深化。私募賺錢效應明顯降溫，多數股票策略年內錄得負收益。不過，根據新財富私募指數資料，管理期貨（CTA）策略成功逆襲，真正跑出了“危機阿爾法”。隨著極致集中的科技行情瓦解，市場正從“單一抱團”向“風格再平衡”過渡，有望重回多點開花的輪動行情。在這種環境下，量化策略相對更容易發揮選股優勢。新財富私募指數顯示，上週CTA策略私募成為市場上唯一錄得正收益的產品策略，正收益佔比超70%，跑贏股票、債券、宏觀等主流策略。拉長時間來看，CTA策略今年以來平均收益近9%，大幅跑贏排名第二的股票中性策略（2%），並且在9月成為主要私募策略中唯一實現正收益的品種。

【SK Hynix abandons US IPO for Solidigm after shareholder opposition, regulatory review】SK Hynix has abandoned plans to list Solidigm in the United States after strong shareholder opposition and regulatory scrutiny over potential harm to minority shareholders, the company said in a regulatory filing on Friday. ### 2026-10-23 07:11:52  #Trading Economics #Economy #United Kingdom #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

UK Consumer Confidence Posts Biggest Drop Since Pandemic — The UK GfK Consumer Confidence Index plunged 11 points to -24 in October 2026 from -13 in September, marking its sharpest monthly decline since April 2020 and its lowest reading since April. The result was significantly worse than market expectations of -14 as rising inflation, higher energy prices and the ongoing Middle East conflict weighed on household sentiment ahead of Finance Minister John Healey's budget next week. Confidence in the economic outlook deteriorated sharply, while willingness to make major purchases fell to its lowest level in more than two years. Inflation expectations also rose to their highest since April, increasing concerns that the Bank of England could raise interest rates in November. The decline reverses a summer recovery, with other surveys also indicating weakening sentiment despite stronger economic growth in the first half of the year. GfK said confidence fell across income, age and gender groups, reflecting household financial pressures.

NZX 50 Poised to End Week on a Low Note — The NZX 50 fell 47 points, or 0.3%, to 13,670 in Friday morning trade, extending losses from the previous session and tracking a fall on Wall Street overnight as a tech-sector selloff deepened. Caution prevailed ahead of the RBNZ’s monetary policy decision next week, as well as the Fed’s interest rate decision. Traders continued to assess Q3 inflation data. Although annual inflation eased to 3.7% in Q3, caution persisted amid expectations that inflation would rise again following a temporary dip in fuel prices. Inflation also remained above the RBNZ’s 1–3% target range. Rising oil prices further pressured sentiment, raising concerns over inflation expectations and interest rate hikes. The technology, consumer discretionary, and financial sectors weighed on the index, with notable losses including Meridian Energy (-1.5%), East Income (-2.4%), EBOS Group (-0.9%), and Summerset Group. For the week, the index is heading for a 0.6% fall after being almost flat in the previous week.

【中小銀行債券投資“攻守換防”，久期策略迴歸審慎】近日，10年期國債收益率持續下行，截至10月22日，已觸及歷史低位1.6414%。機構對長端品種交易態度趨於謹慎。“我們對金融投資規模的控制早已開始，今年以來未新增債券持倉。”西部地區某農商行金融市場部人士坦言。不過，也有中小銀行稱，債券投資業務仍按原有節奏開展。 中小銀行債券投資的底層邏輯正在重塑。過去兩年，不少中小銀行依賴拉長久期、透過交易盤博取波段價差來支撐非息收入。如今，這一模式遭遇三重壓力：監管引導、風控顧慮，以及負債端約束。在此背景下，中小銀行的操作從“賣短買長”切換為“買短賣長”，整體策略由交易主導逐步轉向以票息鎖定和流動性管理為核心的被動配置。（中證報）

【國際貨幣基金組織：預計今年中東地區經濟將萎縮1.3%】國際貨幣基金組織於阿聯酋迪拜釋出《區域經濟展望》。報告指出，受美以伊衝突影響，能源生產、航運、貿易和旅遊等領域受到衝擊，預計2026年中東、北非、阿富汗和巴基斯坦的經濟將萎縮1.3%。國際貨幣基金組織中東和中亞部主任吉哈德·阿祖爾預測，海灣阿拉伯國家合作委員會成員國中，除阿曼外，其餘成員國經濟今年均將出現萎縮。相比之下，石油進口國經濟表現出一定韌性。國際貨幣基金組織預計，如果地區衝突在2027年初緩和，地區貿易逐步恢復正常，中東和北非、阿富汗及巴基斯坦經濟增長水平有望在2027年實現反彈。（央視新聞）

【美國羅得島州起訴“元”公司縱容詐騙廣告】美國羅得島州在州法院對美國“元”公司（Meta）提起訴訟，指控該公司在旗下社交媒體臉書（Facebook）、照片牆（Instagram）和即時通訊應用WhatsApp上對氾濫的詐騙廣告採取縱容態度。該訴訟由羅得島州總檢察長提交，指控Meta透過參與制造詐騙廣告非法牟利。這是美國各州針對Meta提起的首起與詐騙相關的訴訟，此前加利福尼亞州聖克拉拉縣曾在5月份提起過類似訴訟。羅得島州的訴狀引用了去年被披露的Meta內部檔案。根據這些檔案，Meta每天向使用者展示約150億條“高風險”詐騙廣告，其2024年收入的約10%來自欺詐性電子商務、可疑保健品和其他被禁的營銷活動。該訴訟要求對Meta涉嫌違反羅得島州法律的每一項行為處以民事罰款，要求向消費者退還損失，追繳其非法所得，並申請法院判令Meta整改平臺上的詐騙廣告，而不是從中獲利。Meta表示將應訴，稱這起訴訟“毫無根據”，並指出該公司定期與執法部門合作以打擊詐騙網路。Meta發言人稱，僅在過去一年裡，Meta就刪除了超過1.59億條詐騙廣告，並推出了新的反欺詐工具。（央視新聞）

Wall Street Falls as Tech Sell-Off Deepens — US stock indices closed lower on Thursday amid a sell-off in technology stocks. The S&P 500 fell 1.2%, and the Nasdaq lost 2.9%, while the Dow closed flat. Tech stocks were under pressure as corporate earnings failed to impress, renewing concerns over the AI trade, while the rotation away from semiconductor stocks continued. Meta sank 7% despite reporting higher earnings and revenue, as its credit risk reached a record high. Meta plans to ramp up capital spending reignited fears that tech firms will take on more debt to finance AI infrastructure. Tesla dropped 3.1% after scaling back its spending plans, with capital investment in the previous quarter coming in below expectations. Lam Research tumbled 11.8% after issuing a disappointing outlook. Among chipmakers, Nvidia lost 4.4%, Micron shed 6.3%, AMD plunged 8.1%, and Intel fell 8%.

Brent Tops $113 as Middle East Tensions Escalate — Brent crude rose above $113 a barrel on Thursday, its highest level since early May, amid escalating Middle East tensions and reports that the US is planning to resume strikes against Iran. US and Israeli militaries have reportedly drawn up plans for renewed attacks after next month’s midterm elections, with Iran’s energy infrastructure likely to be a key target. The US has also ordered non-emergency government employees and their families to leave Saudi Arabia following escalating attacks by Iran-allied Houthis. The order came after an attack on King Fahd International Airport in Dammam, on Saudi Arabia’s east coast, on Wednesday. Meanwhile, Saudi Aramco has reportedly instructed staff to evacuate its headquarters in Dhahran and work remotely. Aramco facilities have repeatedly come under Houthi attack. The developments increased fears of prolonged disruptions to oil flows from the region.

### 2026-10-23 03:12:20  #Trading Economics #Economy #Argentina #Retail Sales YoY #Importance 1

Argentina’s Retail Sales Surge in August — Argentina’s retail sales surged 24.3% year over year to ARS 698.56 billion in August 2026, following a 23% increase in July. By category, entertainment and leisure recorded the strongest growth at 62.8%, followed by furniture, home décor and textiles (41.6%), sporting goods and accessories (36.4%), and perfumery and pharmacy (33%). Food courts, food outlets and kiosks rose 28.1%, while bookstore and stationery sales and other categories both increased 26.1%. Clothing, footwear and leather goods grew 17%, while toy sales rose 13.5%. Electronics, household appliances and computing was the only category to decline, falling 4%. By region, sales increased 31.6% in the Autonomous City of Buenos Aires and 20.4% across the 24 districts of Greater Buenos Aires. Sales rose 20.1% in the Pampas region and 23.9% in Cuyo, while the Northern and Patagonia regions recorded increases of 21.9% and 32.2%, respectively.

【美國羅德島州起訴Meta】當地時間10月22日，美國羅得島州在州法院對美國“元”公司（Meta）提起訴訟，指控該公司在旗下社交媒體臉書（Facebook）、照片牆（Instagram）和即時通訊應用WhatsApp上對氾濫的詐騙廣告採取縱容態度。該訴訟由羅得島州總檢察長提交，指控Meta透過參與制造詐騙廣告非法牟利。這是美國各州針對Meta提起的首起與詐騙相關的訴訟，此前加利福尼亞州聖克拉拉縣曾在5月份提起過類似訴訟。 羅得島州的訴狀引用了去年被披露的Meta內部檔案。根據這些檔案，Meta每天向使用者展示約150億條“高風險”詐騙廣告，其2024年收入的約10%來自欺詐性電子商務、可疑保健品和其他被禁的營銷活動。 該訴訟要求對Meta涉嫌違反羅得島州法律的每一項行為處以民事罰款，要求向消費者退還損失，追繳其非法所得，並申請法院判令Meta整改平臺上的詐騙廣告，而不是從中獲利。 Meta表示將應訴，稱這起訴訟“毫無根據”，並指出該公司定期與執法部門合作以打擊詐騙網路。

### 2026-10-23 02:40:03  #Trading Economics #Economy #Argentina #Consumer Confidence #Importance 0

Argentina’s Consumer Confidence Rises in October — Argentina’s Consumer Confidence Index rose 0.81% month over month to 41.52 points in October 2026. By region, the index increased 6.77% in the Autonomous City of Buenos Aires, while declining 0.14% in Greater Buenos Aires and 0.25% in the rest of the country. The Interior recorded the highest index level at 46.00 points, while GBA had the lowest at 39.49 points. By income group, confidence among high-income households rose 1.99%, while it fell 1.30% among low-income households. The index stood at 41.76 points for high-income households, compared with 40.75 points for low-income households. Among the subindices, Durable Goods and Real Estate climbed 8.77%, while Personal Situation fell 2.59% and Macroeconomic Situation declined 1.57%. Meanwhile, Present Conditions improved 5.88%, while Future Expectations decreased 2.61%.

【加拿大嚴限駐美外交官接觸中期選舉候選人 以免被指政治干預】知情人士稱，加拿大駐美外交官被要求與參加美國中期選舉的候選人保持距離，並保持低調，以免遭到特朗普政府的政治干預指控。根據週二下發的書面指引，除非獲得特別批准，加拿大官員不得與中期選舉候選人會面，甚至不得與他們同處一室。這些候選人包括所有眾議院候選人以及角逐參議院約三分之一改選席位的候選人。如有必要，加拿大官員仍可開展其他公務，並與此次不參加改選的美國議員會面。不過，據因談論內部備忘錄而要求匿名的知情人士稱，他們已被告知，應確保不會在照片中與任何美國民選官員同框。備忘錄稱這些措施是一種“高度規避風險的做法”。近500名加拿大外交人員在美國工作，包括駐華盛頓大使館以及底特律、洛杉磯、達拉斯和亞特蘭大等城市的12個領事機構。這些措施將持續至11月3日中期選舉投票日。

### 2026-10-23 02:33:38  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Wholesale Prices #Importance 1

Canadian Raw Materials Inflation Exceeds Forecasts — Canada’s Raw Materials Price Index rose 4.8% month over month in September 2026, exceeding forecasts of a 3.1% increase and accelerating from a 3.3% rise in August. Prices for crude energy products surged 14.4%, following a 7.1% increase in August, with conventional crude oil and synthetic crude oil prices rising 16.8% and 9.0%, respectively. Crop product prices increased 2.1%, rebounding from a 0.7% decline in August, driven by higher prices for canola (+3.7%) and other crop products (+1.2%). In contrast, prices for metal ores, concentrates and scrap fell 0.7%, reflecting lower prices for gold, silver and platinum group metal ores and concentrates (-1.2%). Gold ores, concentrates and mill bullion prices declined 1.5%. Prices for animals and animal products decreased 1.1%. Year over year, the Raw Materials Price Index climbed 27%.

【WSJ: Trump called Musk seeking removal of Starlink speed caps for Ukraine】The Wall Street Journal, citing U.S. officials, reported President Trump called Elon Musk this week seeking removal of speed caps on the military version of Starlink used by Ukraine. Officials said Trump complained system speeds were limited to about 100 mph (161 km/h); after the call the network began returning to normal on Thursday, though Ukrainian officials said service had not fully recovered but speeds had improved. U.S. and Ukrainian officials said the caps prevented some attack drones and interceptor drones from operating effectively. Michael Kofman at the Carnegie Endowment for International Peace said lifting the cap would help Ukraine extend strike depth and enable deployment of jet-powered interceptor drones. The White House cited Trump’s prior denials; Trump has denied asking Musk to limit Starlink speeds or knowing who ordered the caps.

【美國10年期國債收益率跌破5% 避險需求推動債市走高】受AI概念股大跌引發的避險買盤推動，美國國債飆升，10年期收益率回落至5%以下。這波漲勢推動各期限美債收益率最多下跌16個基點，5年至30年期收益率均觸及近一個月最低水平。除了避險需求外，市場對美聯儲明年加息的預期降溫也提振了國債走勢。投資者擔心，如果AI投資勢頭減弱，美國經濟增長或難以持續，因此儘管油價再度上漲，資金仍湧入債市。花旗利率衍生品策略師Michael Chang表示：“儘管當天油價上漲，美國國債仍表現出避險資產的特徵”。被視為AI投資情緒風向標的美國半導體公司股價週四恢復下跌，費城半導體指數一度重挫6.3%，至7月底以來最低水平。接下來美債走勢可能部分取決於高油價和對AI股票交易的擔憂哪一個佔據上風。星展銀行高階利率策略師Eugene Leow表示：“隨著投資者重新評估AI相關資產，最近市場情緒似乎搖擺不定。美國國債收益率按近期標準看仍處於高位，投資者可能希望透過買入美債避險，對沖其中一些風險。”

### 2026-10-23 01:24:35  #公司 #市場 #國際

【Moderna、默沙東股價下跌，黑色素瘤疫苗資料疑遭洩露且顯著低於預期】有報道稱，兩家公司備受期待的個性化黑色素瘤疫苗結果已經流出，且治療效果不及預期，Moderna與默沙東（Merck & Co.）股價隨之下跌。兩家公司曾在8月宣佈，這款定製疫苗與默沙東暢銷藥Keytruda聯用，在降低這種致命腫瘤復發方面取得了“顯著且具有臨床意義”的效果。訊息公佈時Moderna股價曾大幅飆升，但當時並未披露具體資料。詳細結果定於本週六（10月24日）在馬德里舉行的歐洲腫瘤內科學會（ESMO）年會上正式公佈。週四，關於該疫苗（名為intismeran autogene）的細節開始從社交平臺上的未經驗證信源流出，Moderna和默沙東股價隨之走低。其中一個賬號釋出了一份署名Oncology Central的報道，稱復發或死亡風險降低了42%。然而，這一數字與投資者記憶中的基準形成了落差。此前一項涉及不同患者群體、不同時間段的早期研究（因而並非可直接比較的對照）顯示，該聯合方案將復發或死亡風險降低了49%，並將遠處轉移（即轉移灶）或死亡的風險降低了59%。42%這一洩露數值明顯低於49%，而III期試驗的完整風險比、置信區間和事件計數等關鍵指標仍未公開。在正式資料缺席的情況下，市場選擇以“已知的更低數字”進行定價。

CAC 40 Falls Amid Fiscal Concerns and Mixed Earnings — The CAC 40 fell 1.4% to close at 7,811 on Thursday amid mixed corporate earnings, rising borrowing costs and political uncertainty. Hermès dropped 4.9% after reporting revenue below estimates. Kering lost 1.3% following a decline in sales. In contrast, L’Oréal gained 1.8% on stronger-than-expected sales growth. The 10-year yield climbed amid concerns over the government’s ability to restore public finances amid ongoing political turmoil and planned protests. Moody’s review of France’s Aa3 sovereign credit rating is due Friday, with its negative outlook raising the risk of a downgrade that could further increase borrowing costs. Financial stocks tumbled, with BNP Paribas falling 4.9% and AXA losing 2.7%. Industrials declined, Safran fell 3.8% and Legrand shed 5.8%. Tech stocks came under pressure as disappointing results from major US technology companies revived concerns over the profitability of heavy AI investments. STMicroelectronics fell 4.9%, while Schneider Electric lost 5.4%.

【歐洲股市收盤下跌 企業財報令人失望且AI憂慮重燃】受愛馬仕等大型公司業績不及預期以及人工智慧相關擔憂再度升溫影響，歐洲股市下跌。歐洲斯托克600指數收盤下跌1%。礦業股和銀行股跌幅居前，食品飲料板塊則上漲。歐洲銀行股指數收於6月初以來最低水平。愛馬仕因亞太地區銷售表現令人失望而下滑。科技股也隨大盤走低，瑞銀編制的一籃子歐洲AI“贏家”股票下跌6.1%。按指數點數計算，阿斯麥成為拖累指數最大的個股，投資者關注AI基礎設施支出的可持續性。隨著AI情緒惡化，西門子能源下跌8.6%；諾基亞下跌9.7%，此前公佈的業績喜憂參半。歐洲股市近幾周走勢停滯，較8月高點下跌3.3%。市場對AI交易的擔憂、法國債券市場拋售以及持續高企的油價都令市場承壓。沙烏地阿拉伯發生致命襲擊後，市場對供應的擔憂加劇，布倫特原油升破每桶111美元。MPPM交易主管Guillermo Hernandez Sampere表示：“本輪企業財報有可能推動市場擺脫當前交易區間，前提是歐元區內部的風險能夠得到控制。”

### 2026-10-23 00:41:58  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Producer Price Inflation MoM #Importance 1

Canada Producer Prices Rise the Most in 6 Months — Producer prices in Canada rose 2.0% month-on-month in September 2026, accelerating from a downwardly revised 1.2% gain in August and contrasting with market expectations of a slowdown to 0.9%. This marked the sharpest monthly rise since March, driven primarily by the prolonged US-Iran conflict, which continued to push inflation of energy and petroleum products higher to 12.1% following a 3.7% gain in August. The increase reflected higher prices for refined products (13.7%), particularly diesel (18.4%), followed by finished motor gasoline (7.8%) and light fuel oils (15.4%). Excluding energy and petroleum products, producer prices edged up 0.3%. Chemical and chemical product prices rebounded sharply by 3.8% after falling 0.3% in August, driven by higher prices for dyes and pigments and petrochemicals (9.7%). Plastic resins (11.0%) and fertilizers, pesticides and other chemical products (4.1%) also contributed significantly to the increase.

【西藏巴青縣發生7.0級地震 暫未收到人員傷亡報告】據中國地震臺網測定，10月22日22時33分在西藏那曲市巴青縣（北緯32.40度，東經93.79度）發生7.0級地震，震源深度10千米。記者核實瞭解到，巴青縣縣城震感強烈，電力中斷，訊號不暢。目前暫無人員傷亡報告。 據瞭解，震中位於巴青縣瑪如鄉，距離縣城70公里左右。瑪如鄉副鄉長拉旺達布說，鄉政府震感明顯，辦公樓有牆體開裂，有房屋倒塌情況，各村組情況正在排查中。 記者核實瞭解到，西藏昌都市各地震感明顯，邊壩、丁青等距離震中較近縣區的高層住戶已到室外避險，學校組織學生到操場集中，全市暫未收到財產損失和人員傷亡報告。昌都市應急管理局黨委書記溫雲春介紹，昌都市已組織80人的專業救援力量前往丁青縣、洛隆縣、邊壩縣區域開展排查。此外，林芝市巴宜區震感明顯，墨脫縣、米林市、朗縣等方向有輕微震感。（新華社）

### 2026-10-23 00:17:34  #公司 #市場

【蘋果股價與晶片股走勢高度背離 成為對沖AI概念股的理想選擇】隨著市場對人工智慧(AI)交易的情緒劇烈波動，股市中逐漸形成了一種頗為穩定的交易模式：無論AI概念股怎麼走，蘋果公司往往反向而行。這家iPhone製造商股價2026年以來上漲了28%，成為標普500指數12%漲幅的最大貢獻者。蘋果也是今年“七巨頭”科技股中表現最好的公司，較2025年形成鮮明反差。當時蘋果全年僅上漲8%，在該類股票中接近墊底。這種分化的原因相對直接。多數大型科技公司正大舉投入AI基礎設施，而蘋果基本沒有參與這場資本開支競賽，反而繼續專注於其一貫擅長的領域，即打造能夠吸引消費者關注的產品，例如新推出的摺疊屏iPhone Duo。隨著華爾街對AI資本支出的看法不斷變化，蘋果股票已成為投資者繼續配置大型科技股、同時規避部分波動風險的一種偏好選擇。管理4180億美元資產的Russell Investments高階投資組合經理Jordan McCall表示：“蘋果是對沖AI波動性的一種流動性很高的工具。如果你開始擔心AI，但仍希望保留科技股敞口，蘋果是很不錯的持倉。”他說：“當市場變得緊張時，蘋果會成為資金尋求安全的去處。”

### 2026-10-23 00:12:54  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #30 Year Mortgage Rate #Importance 1

US Mortgage Rates See First Drop in 9 Weeks — The US 30-year fixed-rate mortgage averaged 7.32% as of October 22, 2026, remaining above the 7% threshold, but seeing a slight decline from its highest level since November 2023 reached over the previous two weeks. This marks the first decline in nine weeks, reflecting the downward trend seen in US Treasury yields over the past two weeks, where expectations of higher-for-longer interest rates lifted safe-haven demand from capital-intensive sectors. Mortgage rates tend to closely track these these sovereign yields. A year ago, the the 30-year fixed-rate mortgage averaged 6.19%. Meanwhile, the 15-year fixed-rate mortgage averaged 6.68%, down from the 6.71% reached a week prior.

DAX Closes in the Red — Frankfurt's DAX 40 closed about 1.1% down at 25,379 on Thursday, pulling back from one-month highs, tracking other European peers. Rising oil prices, geopolitical risks, concerns over AI infrastructure spending, and uncertainty in France weighed on investor sentiment. Meanwhile, the earnings season continued across both sides of the Atlantic. Technology, industrial, and banking sectors led the declines, while software and telecom stocks outperformed. German companies benefiting from AI, such as Infineon, Hochtief, Siemens Energy Siemens plunged between 3.9%-8.1%. Other top losers included MTU Aero Engines (-3.9%), Commerzbank (-3.8%), Deutsche Bank (-2.8%), RWE (-2.3%) and Airbus (-2.2%). On the upside, shares of Qiagen NV (4.9%) and Scout24 (3.6%) stood out. Software giant SAP gained 3.2%, after quarterly revenue exceeded €10 billion for the first time, supported by 26% growth in cloud revenue, prompting the company to raise its full-year revenue outlook.

### 2026-10-23 00:04:07  #市場 #央行 #國際

【歐洲央行管委帕內塔：債券收益率上升將抑制經濟增長】歐洲央行管理委員會成員Fabio Panetta表示，債券收益率躍升將抑制該地區經濟，不過就目前情況而言並不嚴重。這位義大利央行行長在都靈發表講話時稱，債券市場的走勢是一種全球現象，過去一週主要由經濟增長和通脹均超過預期的美國引領。“問題在於，在美國，利率上升伴隨著更強勁的增長，而歐洲的增長則沒有那麼強勁，”他在週三表示。“這不是一場危機，但確實會拖累增長。”與其他歐洲央行官員一樣，Panetta警告稱金融環境收緊可能會限制經濟擴張，儘管這種放緩也可能有助於遏制通脹。能源成本飆升已推動歐元區通脹率升至接近2%目標的兩倍。預計歐洲央行決策者下週將維持利率不變。他們目前正處於會議前為期一週的靜默期，在此期間不能就貨幣政策發表評論。在由21個國家組成的歐元區內，市場壓力主要集中在法國，這個歐元區第二大成員國正艱難地試圖讓公共財政走上可持續軌道。Panetta表示，由於公共財政狀況更為穩健，德國債券收益率的升幅較小。他特別指出，法國公共債務和預算赤字持續處於高位，並稱主權債務緊張局勢可能會影響義大利的借貸成本。“當美國長期利率上升時，我們會看到這種影響外溢至其他市場，包括歐洲，”Panetta說。“這也會影響義大利的融資成本。”

FTSE 100 Edges Down on Thursday — The FTSE 100 fell on Thursday, although losses were smaller than across other European markets, as gains in pharmaceutical and consumer stocks partly offset declines in banks and mining companies. Antofagasta dropped over 6%, while Fresnillo and Anglo American each lost around 5.5%, and Endeavour fell more than 4%. Barclays declined over 2% after announcing plans to cut 5,000 jobs through AI-driven efficiencies, while its bond-trading performance disappointed in the third quarter. UK bank shares also weakened amid growing concerns over higher taxes in next week’s budget, with HSBC and Lloyds falling over 2%, NatWest losing more than 1% and Standard Chartered dropping over 4%. J Sainsbury slipped over 2% after reporting weaker-than-expected first-half like-for-like sales growth. In contrast, AstraZeneca and GSK each gained 2.6%, Unilever rose 3%, and British American Tobacco advanced more than 2%, providing some support to the broader index.

European Stocks Sink on Thursday — European stocks fell sharply on Thursday, with the Stoxx 50 dropping around 2% to a more than four-month low and the broader Stoxx 600 losing 1% to a one-week low. Italy’s market declined over 2.5%, Spain fell around 2%, while Germany and France lost more than 1% and the UK slipped less than 0.5%. Basic resources plunged over 3.5% as metals prices tumbled, including silver down nearly 5%, copper over 2.5% and gold more than 2%. Banks fell over 3%, while industrial goods and services lost around 2.5% and technology declined 2%. Energy and travel stocks also weakened. In contrast, health care and food, beverage & tobacco each gained around 2%. Among individual stocks, Hermès dropped 5% after reporting flat sales, while Thales and Barclays declined despite raising its outlook and reporting higher profits, respectively. SAP rose over 3.5% after upgrading its full-year outlook, while Nestlé gained 2.3% on strong quarterly sales. Roche was little changed despite better-than-expected sales.

### 2026-10-22 23:50:45  #Trading Economics #Markets #Italy #Stock Market #Importance 0

Italian Stocks Plunge to May Low — Italy’s FTSE MIB closed 2.5% lower to 48,384 on Thursday, nearing its lowest level since May, as banking and tech stocks led losses. AI-related shares came under pressure amid renewed valuation concerns, with Technopobe (-7.6%), Prysmian (-7.6%) and STMicroelectronics (-5%) dropping sharply. Protests by truck drivers and farmers in southern Italy also added pressure, causing shortages of food and industrial components and prompting the government to approve additional spending to aid diesel purchases. The BTP-Bund spread widened to 126 bps, reflecting a growing risk premium and weighing on banks, with UniCredit down 4.7%. Stellantis was also affected, falling 0.6% after halting production at three regional plants. European gas prices approached a four-year high, pressuring utilities, while Italgas slipped 1%. Energy firms also declined as oil prices edged lower, while Saipem fell 6% while signing a preliminary agreement with Egypt’s MDC to explore offshore drilling projects in Egypt.

### 2026-10-22 23:46:19  #MKT News

【ECB's Panetta: Rising bond yields will curb growth】ECB executive board member Panetta said the recent surge in bond yields will weigh on the region's economy but is not yet a critical problem. He called the moves global and US-led over the past week, with US growth and inflation beating expectations. "The issue is that higher US rates have coincided with stronger growth while Europe's growth is relatively weak," he said. Panetta and other ECB officials warned tighter financial conditions could constrain expansion but may help rein in inflation that has risen to roughly double target levels after energy-cost shocks. He said Germany's yields rose less due to stronger public finances, flagged France's persistently high public debt and deficits, and warned sovereign stress could push up Italy's borrowing costs. "When US long-term yields rise, we see spillovers to other markets including Europe," he added, noting that will affect Italy's financing costs.

【巴克萊將裁員5000人 藉助人工智慧削減成本】巴克萊將裁減5000個崗位，以藉助人工智慧降低成本，成為又一家因新技術逐漸滲透整個銀行業而縮減員工規模的大型銀行。此次裁員相當於這家總部位於倫敦的銀行總員工人數的約5%，其中約60%的削減來自支援職能，包括開發人員。5000個崗位中包括該行上半年已削減的1000個職位。此前巴克萊今年早些時候公佈的費用高於預期，此次裁員是其控制成本舉措的一部分。這家英國銀行加入滙豐控股和渣打集團的行列，轉向使用人工智慧和自動化，也更加清楚地顯示出新技術正在如何重塑傳統銀行業工作。滙豐正考慮未來幾年最多裁減2萬個崗位，渣打則計劃到2030年將企業職能崗位削減逾15%。越來越多跡象顯示，人工智慧正在深入金融機構。德意志銀行執行長Christian Sewing週三表示，該行正在利用AI提高效率並加強管控。

Brent Crude Approaches War-Driven Highs — Brent crude rose toward $112 a barrel on Thursday, approaching the four-year high of $114.44 reached on May 4, amid concerns that the Middle East conflict could escalate as the US reportedly prepares to resume strikes against Iran. US and Israeli military officials have developed plans for a renewed campaign following next month’s US midterm elections, with preparations for a short but intensive operation underway. President Donald Trump said Washington was not negotiating with Tehran and suggested that developments could occur soon, potentially after the elections. Meanwhile, the US ordered non-emergency government employees and their families to leave Saudi Arabia, citing escalating attacks by Iran-aligned Houthis on civilian targets. Despite rising tensions, crude exports through the Strait of Hormuz have moved closer to pre-conflict levels, with some tankers reportedly disabling tracking systems to avoid detection. However, refined oil product shipments remain constrained.

### 2026-10-22 23:12:49  #Trading Economics #Economy #United States #Kansas Fed Manufacturing Index #Importance 1

Kansas Fed Manufacturing Activity Growth Slows — The Kansas City Fed’s Manufacturing Production Index eased to 16 in October 2026 from 20 in September, which was the highest reading since April 2022. Growth in both durable and nondurable manufacturing activity slowed slightly, but there was strong growth in printing activities. Most month-over-month indexes remained in positive territory, with the exceptions of the backlog of orders index, which fell to -5 from 13, and the new export orders index, which declined to -9 from 0. Although most indexes posted lower readings than in September, the employment, supplier delivery time, and materials inventory indexes increased. On the price front, growth in raw material prices continued to accelerate, while price growth for finished goods eased. Meanwhile, expectations for future activity edged higher, reaching their strongest levels since July 2022.

### 2026-10-22 23:03:55  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

US Natgas Prices Edge Up from 2-Month Low — US natural gas prices rebounded to $2.81 per MMBtu, recovering slightly from a two-month low of $2.79 recorded on October 21, after the EIA reported a smaller-than-expected weekly increase in storage inventories. Lower production earlier this month likely contributed to reduced gas injections, with energy companies adding 77 billion cubic feet (bcf) to storage in the week ended October 16, below market expectations of 79 bcf. The increase was also lower than the 86 bcf added during the same week last year and the five-year average of 87 bcf. Total inventories reached 3.644 trillion cubic feet, 4% below year-earlier levels but 1% above the five-year average. Meanwhile, average natural gas output across the Lower 48 states declined from record highs of 113.3 billion cubic feet per day (bcfd) in August and September to 111.6 bcfd so far in October. In contrast, average gas flows to the nine major US LNG export facilities increased to 18.1 bcfd in October, up from 17.9 bcfd in September.

US Natural Gas Stocks Rise Less Than Expected — US energy companies added 77 billion cubic feet (bcf) of natural gas to storage in the week ended October 16, falling short of market expectations for an injection of 79 bcf. The increase was also below the 86 bcf added during the same period last year and the five-year average build of 87 bcf for the week from 2021 to 2025. Total gas inventories rose to 3.644 trillion cubic feet, standing 4% below year-earlier levels but 1% above the five-year average for this time of year. The latest figures followed an injection of 67 bcf in the previous week ended October 9, indicating that storage additions accelerated on a weekly basis despite remaining weaker than historical norms and analyst forecasts.

### 2026-10-22 22:50:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Cocoa Futures Drop — Cocoa futures fell over 4% to around $5,500 a tonne, reaching their lowest since early October, as investors trimmed long positions following recent gains, while rising inventories also added to the pressure. ICE cocoa inventories have steadily expanded since the start of the year, reaching a 2.5-year high of 3,635,354 bags by October 21. Still, signs of improving demand and concerns over West African supplies limted the downside. Cocoa grindings across key processing regions rose 14.3% in Q3, beating market expectations as Asian processing jumped 29.2% and European volumes increased by 3.2%. North America was the exception, recording a 2.5% decline. On the supply side, concerns are mounting over the 2026/27 West African cocoa crop. Below-average rainfall in the Ivory Coast has raised fears of weaker pod development, while Ghana is also facing a potentially smaller harvest amid weather-related stress and crop disease. The expected strong impact of El Niño adds further uncertainty.

### 2026-10-22 22:46:17  #其他

【我國將培育500家國家級零碳工廠】我國推動零碳工廠建設，並不是要求企業的現階段就要做到絕對零排放，而是透過技術創新，結構調整，管理最佳化，把碳排放真正減下來，持續降低並逐步趨向於零。入選國家級零碳工廠建設的名單，也並不意味著已經建成了零碳工廠，還需要按照建設方案來持續開展技術改造，最終經過評估驗收。核心指標既看碳排放降到了什麼水平，也要看能源的結構發生了什麼變化。 《關於開展零碳工廠建設工作的指導意見》明確實施分階段梯度培育。2026年起，我國開始遴選一批零碳工廠，發揮標杆示範作用；到2027年，優先在汽車、鋰電池、光伏、電子電器、輕工、機械、算力設施等脫碳難度相對較小的行業，培育建設一批零碳工廠；到2030年，建設範圍將逐步拓展到鋼鐵、有色金屬、石化化工、建材、紡織等高載能行業，探索傳統產業脫碳新路徑。  近日，工信部印發《加快構建工業和資訊化領域碳排放雙控工作體系實施方案（2026—2030年）》，提出到2030年，制定實施重點工業產品碳足跡核算規則標準400項左右，建設國家級零碳工廠和零碳算力設施500個左右，規模以上工業單位增加值二氧化碳排放比2025年降低17%以上。 （央視財經）

【12035公里遠端手術延遲不到200毫秒 前8月#國產手術機器人出口暴漲272.9%】近幾年，我國機器人產業蓬勃發展，而手術機器人這一專業賽道更是逐步打破了國外品牌的壟斷，進入快速發展期。中國手術機器人市場規模預計將從2026年的224.5億元增至2030年的708.5億元，複合增長率達到33.3%。  在國內市場實現國產替代突破的同時，國產手術機器人出海步伐持續加快。今年前8個月，全國手術機器人出口大幅增長，廣東出口同比增4倍。 廣東深圳一家手術機器人研發製造企業2025年開始拓展手術機器人出口業務，公司2026年上半年財報顯示，海外市場收入佔比已經達到70%，產品遠銷亞太和歐洲多國。據介紹，2025年9月，這家公司的腔鏡類手術機器人完成了一場巴西到科威特的跨洲際遠端手術，兩地距離12035公里，通訊延遲不到200毫秒。 憑藉高價效比、高品質、強迭代的特質，國產手術機器人正加速出海。海關資料顯示，今年1-8月，我國手術機器人出口金額6.72億元，同比增長272.9%。 （央視財經）

### 2026-10-22 22:30:15  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 0

Palladium Drops Further to a 16-Month Low — Palladium futures extended its decline to below $1,100 an ounce and reaching its lowest level since June 2025, weighed by persistent investment outflows and a bearish long-term demand outlook. A prolonged Middle East conflict has driven global energy prices higher as risk premiums rise, fuelling inflation and strengthening expectations of hawkish central bank decisions across economies. Over the longer term, higher rates could exert greater downward pressure in investment demand for the metal that carries no yield. Higher borrowing costs and fuel prices also weigh on automotive demand, while the shift towards platinum in catalytic converters adds to long-term demand concerns, although resilient hybrid demand has helped cushion the decline. Meanwhile, the lack of major disruptions in key producing countries and steady recycling flows eased concerns about shortages. Market sentiment is also undermined by a projected surplus in the platinum group metals complex.

### 2026-10-22 22:28:49  #公司 #觀點 #國際

【美國銀行業初級崗位數量減少 AI影響或正顯現】美國銀行業職業晉升階梯的第一層正在變窄。勞動力市場資料公司Revelio Labs的報告顯示，根據對包括領英在內的公開就業記錄的彙總並分析，發現自ChatGPT於2022年推出以來，美國銀行業初級崗位數量減少了近5%，相比之下，中高階崗位數量繼續增長。Revelio的資料顯示，在摩根大通、美國銀行和花旗集團等大型銀行，初級崗位與中高階崗位的變化趨勢出現明顯分化。但有兩個顯著例外：富國銀行的初級和更高階別崗位均少於2022年，而高盛這兩個級別的員工數量都有所增加。美國銀行在一份宣告中表示，該行“非常致力於”招聘初級員工，過去兩年每年都招聘了逾14,000名處於職業生涯早期階段的員工，2026年也有望達到同樣規模。摩根大通表示，由於不清楚報告採用的資料和定義，無法驗證其結論。該行稱，過去三年初級員工人數一直保持穩定，而且分析招聘資訊可能會低估實際招聘人數，因為一則招聘資訊可能對應多名錄用者，尤其是在軟體工程和運營等領域的初級崗位。

【第十九屆中國—歐盟投資貿易科技合作洽談會在成都開幕】10月22日上午，由中國國際商會、中國歐盟商會共同主辦的第十九屆中國—歐盟投資貿易科技合作洽談會在成都開幕。中國貿促會副會長、中國國際商會副會長劉健男出席開幕式並致辭。劉健男表示，歐洽會不僅為中歐企業搭建了精準對接的務實平臺，更成為中歐經貿合作互利共贏的生動縮影。中國貿促會、中國國際商會將繼續發揮聯通政企、融通內外、暢通供需功能，進一步釋放歐洽會平臺的輻射帶動效應，推動中歐務實經貿合作行穩致遠，為推動高水平對外開放、建設開放型世界經濟不斷作出新貢獻。會議期間，劉健男還出席了“連結歐洲”投資與合作交流會、中歐貿促機構及商協會圓桌交流會，並與參會各界代表就利用歐洽會平臺深化中歐經貿合作、加強產業鏈供應鏈協同發展等議題進行深入交流。（中國貿促）

【PHP重大漏洞致日本多網站暫停服務】廣泛用於網站開發的PHP指令碼語言21日被曝光存在嚴重安全漏洞後，日本政府22日呼籲各網站運營方採取措施，日本中央和地方政府部門、鐵路、金融機構的多個網站暫停服務。PHP是一種廣泛使用的伺服器端指令碼語言。它在伺服器上執行，用於生成網頁內容、與資料庫互動，也被大量整合到其他產品中。此次安全問題涉及兩個漏洞，攻擊者可能透過觸發記憶體耗盡狀態，造成記憶體損壞，並在特定條件下利用漏洞遠端執行程式碼。21日，PHP開發團隊釋出緊急安全更新，漏洞已被修復。（新華社）

【程式語言PHP嚴重漏洞引發全球緊急修復 多地網站暫時下線】圍繞一種廣泛使用的程式語言PHP所曝出的漏洞，全球網站加緊更新並採取防護措施的行動進入第二天。研究人員警告稱，駭客已經嘗試利用這些漏洞發動攻擊。安全問題造成的影響始於週三，此前研究人員披露，他們在PHP中發現了兩個嚴重漏洞，使用PHP搭建網站和軟體平臺的機構可能因此面臨網路攻擊風險。英國太空、網路與監管改革大臣Elizabeth Lloyd週四在提交給英國上議院的一份宣告中表示，在部署安全更新期間，英國仍有30多項政府服務處於下線狀態。她表示，“在完成相關修復工作後，我們正與各部門一道謹慎推進服務恢復工作，確保服務安全恢復。”電信運營商Orange週四在一份宣告中表示，在為相關漏洞部署安全補丁期間，公司暫停了部分網站和應用程式的訪問。PHP是網際網路上應用最廣泛的技術之一，為數以百萬計的網站提供支援，多達70%的網際網路網站使用這項技術作為底層支撐。因此，任何漏洞都可能沿著網際網路軟體供應鏈產生連鎖影響，使線上服務面臨資料竊取、惡意軟體攻擊和安全入侵等風險。暫時關閉網站可以防止攻擊者在漏洞修復期間接觸並利用漏洞。負責維護PHP的開發人員週三釋出了修復這些漏洞的軟體更新，並敦促使用者立即進行更新。

【我國水力資源蘊藏量世界第一 西電東送帶動數十萬就業崗位】透過特高壓直流通道，將西南地區水風光一體化基地的綠色電能送至中東部地區的負荷中心，是我國“西電東送”戰略的重要組成部分。我國水力資源豐富，蘊藏量超過6萬億千瓦時，居世界第一。其中西南地區水能資源最為豐富，可開發量約佔全國七成，且風光資源條件優越。大渡河、雅礱江、金沙江、瀾滄江等流域水電調節能力強，可以與風光的波動性進行互補，實現水風光一體化開發利用。近期開始投產的雙江口電站，是大渡河流域的龍頭電站，具備年調節能力，可將豐水期來水儲存到枯水期發電，整體提升下游各電站調節能力。截至2026年9月底，西南地區累計建成12回特高壓直流工程，輸電能力接近8500萬千瓦。  電力規劃設計總院副院長何肇介紹：“將西部地區的資源優勢轉化為經濟優勢，同時拉動新能源、輸變電裝備製造上下游產業發展，帶動產業投資數千億元、創造數十萬個就業崗位。從環境角度看，可有效替代中東部地區燃煤發電量，每年預計可減少二氧化碳排放約3億噸。 ”（央視財經）

### 2026-10-22 21:53:04  #宏觀 #國際

【王毅同剛果（布）外長邦達會談】
2026年10月22日，中共中央政治局委員、外交部長王毅在京同剛果（布）外長邦達舉行會談。

王毅表示，習近平主席和薩蘇總統建立了牢固互信和友誼，為中剛關係發展提供了重要政治保障，中剛關係發展勢頭強勁。剛果（布）是第一個同中國簽署共同發展經濟夥伴關係協定早期收穫安排的非洲國家，兩國各領域合作取得積極成果。中方讚賞剛方恪守一箇中國原則、支援中方維護核心利益的正當立場。中方也繼續支援剛方走出符合國情的發展道路，把國家命運掌握在自己手中，反對一切外部勢力的霸凌。

王毅說，中方願在兩國元首戰略指引下，同剛方規劃好下階段高層交往，加強戰略對接，穩步推進務實合作，推動兩國關係邁上新臺階。中剛積極履行中非合作論壇共同主席國職責，推動北京峰會成果落實進展率接近100％。雙方應繼續密切協調，籌備好預定於2027年在剛召開的第十屆部長級會議，推進中非合作的新一輪提質升級。

王毅表示，當前聯合國作用遭遇挑戰，單邊主義、保護主義盛行，國際秩序受到嚴重衝擊。中方支援非洲國家在國際舞臺上發揮更大作用，願同非方一道堅定維護發展中國家正當權益，推進國際關係實現民主化，構建符合南方國家共同願望、更加公正合理的全球治理體系。剛果（布）是國際調解院創始締約國和世界人工智慧合作組織創始成員國。雙方要用好這些多邊平臺，加強溝通協調，維護公平正義。

邦達表示，中國是剛果（布）的好朋友、好兄弟，雙方始終相互信任、相互支援。剛方願同中方在兩國元首指引下，深化兩國各領域合作，推動剛中高水平命運共同體建設。剛方支援習近平主席提出的系列全球倡議，重申堅定恪守一箇中國原則。當前國際地區局勢複雜嚴峻，和平安全受到衝擊，全球南方有必要加強團結合作。中國實施的零關稅政策使包括剛方在內的非洲國家廣泛受益。剛方讚賞中方重視非洲健康衛生事業，協助非洲抗擊埃博拉疫情、加強衛生基礎設施建設。剛方願同中方共同籌備好中非合作論壇新一屆部長級會議，推動非中關係實現新的更大發展。（外交部）

### 2026-10-22 21:50:17  #市場 #央行 #國際

【美聯儲加息時點難料 美國國庫券市場交易愈發棘手】在美聯儲政策路徑和國債供應增加均充滿不確定性的背景下，投資者對希望持有的美國國庫券變得越來越挑剔，而且這種局面短期內可能不會改變。隨著財政赤字擴大，國庫券一直是美國政府融資的重要工具。但流入貨幣市場的資金已經放緩，引發市場這樣一種擔憂：作為國庫券最大買家的一些投資者，能否繼續消化不斷增加的發行量。儘管迄今買家仍能夠吸收新增供應，但期限最短的美國國債市場已經開始出現一些承壓跡象：利差擴大，近期一次拍賣遭遇創紀錄低迷的需求，而承擔為拍賣提供兜底等職能的交易商，其國債持倉已膨脹至2024年以來最高水平。這些顯示市場潛在不穩定性的跡象出現之際，交易員正糾結於資金應該投向何處、鎖定多長期限，因為美聯儲下一次加息的時間仍不明朗。Jefferies首席經濟學家Thomas Simons表示，“如果你是短端交易員，過去一個月會非常棘手。”
