Many market participants believe a Flávio administration would be more likely to rein in public spending, although neither the candidate nor his economic advisers have outlined concrete fiscal adjustments. Datafolha is scheduled to release a new presidential voting-intention poll after the market close today. Petrobras (+1%) helped limit losses in the index, supported by a resume in the oil rally.

### 2026-10-22 21:49:18  #市場

【AI 支出恐慌蔓延，蘋果成為華爾街首選避險標的】隨著人工智慧板塊情緒劇烈波動，股市裡出現了一條可靠交易策略：無論 AI 概念股漲跌，蘋果股價都會走出反向行情。這家 iPhone 製造商 2026 年股價累計上漲 28%，是標普 500 指數年內 12% 漲幅的最大貢獻來源。同時，它也是今年 “科技七雄” 中表現最好的個股，與 2025 年形成鮮明反差 —— 當年蘋果僅上漲 8%，在七雄中排名墊底。股價分化的原因十分清晰。絕大多數大型科技企業大舉投入 AI 基建，蘋果卻基本保持克制，繼續深耕自身傳統優勢：打造引爆消費市場的硬體產品，例如新款摺疊機型 iPhone Duo。市場對 AI 資本開支的預期反覆搖擺，蘋果股票就此成為投資者佈局科技板塊、規避 AI 行情劇烈震盪的首選標的。羅素投資資深投資組合經理 Jordan McCall 表示：“蘋果流動性充足，是對沖 AI 波動的優質工具。如果投資者開始擔憂 AI 行情，但又不想撤出科技賽道，配置蘋果是絕佳選擇。每當市場陷入恐慌，資金就會湧入蘋果避險。” 該機構資產管理規模達 4180 億美元，旗下多隻產品均持有蘋果股票。

Baltic Dry Index Rises for 4th Day — The Baltic Exchange’s dry bulk freight index, which tracks rates for ships carrying dry bulk commodities, advanced for a fourth straight session on Thursday, rising by 0.5% to a fresh high since October 13 at 2,693 points. The capesize index, which typically transports 150,000-ton cargoes, including iron ore and coal, also advanced for a fourth day, up 1.5% to a more than one-week high of 3,848 points. Concurrently, the supramax index increased by 1.1% to a peak since August 2022 at 1,890 points. Meanwhile, the panamax index, which usually carries 60,000 to 70,000 tons of coal or grain, fell further by 2.4% to its lowest since August 24 at 2,151 points.

US Stocks Lower, Meta Sinks — US stocks fell on Thursday, with the S&P 500 down 1.3%, the Nasdaq losing 2.2% and the Dow Jones declining nearly 110 points. Tech stocks remained under pressure as corporate earnings failed to impress investors, fueling renewed concerns over the AI trade, while the rotation away from semiconductor stocks continued. Communication services and consumer discretionary were also under heavy pressure while consumer staples outperformed. Meanwhile, oil prices climbed amid renewed supply risks and escalating geopolitical tensions. Meta tumbled 5.8% despite reporting higher earnings and revenue, while Tesla fell 0.8% after scaling back its spending plans, as capital investment in the previous quarter came in below expectations. Lam Research tumbled more than 10% after issuing a disappointing outlook. Nvidia dropped 3.9%, while IBM lost 1.3%. In addition, Honeywell fell 0.1% despite raising its full-year outlook. In contrast, Lockheed Martin gained 6.6% after beating on earnings and revenue.

### 2026-10-22 21:35:24  #國際

【俄方：尚未確定參與俄美烏三方會談的代表團人選】俄羅斯總統新聞秘書佩斯科夫22日說，俄總統普京尚未確定參與俄羅斯、美國和烏克蘭三方會談的俄方代表團人選。阿布扎比可能成為三方會談的地點。 佩斯科夫當天在每日例行吹風會上回答媒體提問時說，由於調解烏克蘭問題存在複雜性，因此“擴音器外交”和對調解方案的公開討論都只會阻礙談判程序。 佩斯科夫表示，俄方尚未聽到歐洲方面任何“有意停止升級局勢或顧及俄方利益”的表態，歐洲在很大程度上助長了軍事行動的升級。 佩斯科夫還說，在與美國總統特使威特科夫的會晤中，普京未向美方提供俄方關於解決烏克蘭問題的任何書面提案。 俄副外長里亞布科夫當天表示，存在有關俄美烏三方元首會談的話題，相關安排正在討論中，但尚未達成全面協議。他在談及普京與威特科夫的會晤時說，俄方正在認真分析美方帶來的建議，同時也希望美方認真考慮會晤中俄方談到的內容，並將其告知“那些至今仍不願推動調解烏克蘭問題的人”。 俄總統助理烏沙科夫21日通報說，普京當天在與威特科夫會晤時說，俄方準備於11月中旬重啟俄美烏三方會談，會談主題涉及衝突結束後俄烏安全體系建設。(新華社)

【卡達簽約採購美國液化天然氣至 2054 年，戰事衝擊本土供氣】卡達能源公司與 Cheniere Energy 達成協議，每年採購 400 萬噸液化天然氣，合同有效期至 2054 年。受伊朗相關戰事影響，卡達本土產能受限，這是該國首筆美國 LNG 長期採購協議。卡達曾是全球最大液化天然氣出口國之一。該國拉斯拉凡設施在衝突中受損，卡達需要保障那些供貨中斷客戶的氣源；同時關鍵的霍爾木茲海峽航運局勢動盪，卡達能源被迫大幅縮減外運規模，在此背景下達成本次交易。卡達能源週四公告稱，將從 Cheniere 旗下兩座工廠每年各採購 200 萬噸液化天然氣，分別是路易斯安那州的 Sabine Pass 專案和得克薩斯州的 Corpus Christi 專案。供貨將於 2030 年代初啟動，持續至 2054 年。

Mexico Jobless Rate Hits Lowest Since May — Mexico’s unemployment rate fell to 2.8% in September 2026 from 3.0% in August, slightly below forecasts of 3.0% and reaching its lowest level since May. The unemployed population totaled 1.76 million people, with the jobless rate at 2.7% for women and 2.9% for men. By age group, people aged 25–44 accounted for the largest share of the unemployed population, at 46.8%, followed by those aged 15–24, at 30.5%. Regarding unemployment duration, 40% had been out of work for one month or less, while 34.1% had been unemployed for more than one month and up to three months. Meanwhile, 7.2% had been without work for more than six months and up to one year, and 3.3% had been unemployed for more than a year.

South African Rand Weakens — The South African rand weakened to around 16.5 per US dollar on Thursday, giving back some of its recent gains after reaching one-month highs. Deteriorating risk sentiment amid mounting geopolitical tensions pushed oil prices higher, weighing on the currency given South Africa’s reliance on fuel imports. At the same time, falling prices of key precious metals, including gold and PGMs, removed another source of support. Meanwhile, investors continued to assess the government's medium-term budget review, which pointed to improved public finances and reaffirmed the government’s commitment to fiscal discipline. Finance Minister Enoch Godongwana also announced temporary fuel levy cuts to ease cost-of-living pressures. Latest data showed headline consumer inflation ticked up to 4.5% yoy in September from 4.4% in August, moving further away from the central bank's 3% target but below the expected 4.7%. Economists expect a sharper acceleration in October after fuel prices hit a record high.

Stronger-than-expected US consumer and producer inflation data, alongside falling weekly jobless claims, pointed to persistent price pressures and a resilient labor market, reinforcing expectations of tighter monetary policy. While the Fed is widely expected to hold rates at its October 27–28 meeting, Goldman Sachs anticipates another 25-basis-point hike in December, with markets pricing in an over 85% probability of an increase, according to CME’s FedWatch Tool. Investors now await next week’s PCE price index, the Fed’s preferred inflation gauge, for further clues on the interest rate outlook.

US Jobless Claims Fall to Lowest Level Since July — Initial claims for unemployment benefits in the US fell by 6,000 to 194,000 in the week ended October 17, below market expectations of 200,000 and down from a revised 200,000 in the previous week, according to the US Department of Labor. Claims reached their lowest level since mid-July, signaling continued labor market resilience despite mounting economic headwinds. Robust corporate profits and resilient domestic demand are helping shield workers from layoffs, while employers appear reluctant to make significant staffing changes amid heightened uncertainty. Elevated energy costs, driven by the US-Israeli war with Iran, along with uncertainty surrounding the conflict, the outlook for Federal Reserve interest rates and unsettled trade policy that could push business costs higher, are weighing on hiring decisions. Meanwhile, continuing claims rose by 25,000 to 1.728 million in the week ended October 10.

### 2026-10-22 20:16:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Canada #Small Business Sentiment #Importance 2

Canada Small Business Sentiment Improves in October — Canada’s CFIB Business Barometer long-term index, measuring 12-month business expectations, rose to 50 in October 2026 from 48.5 in September, reaching neutral territory. Sentiment improved in agriculture and finance but weakened in construction, manufacturing, retail and transportation. Short-term optimism, based on the three-month outlook, also edged up to 45.3 from 44.1, supported by stronger sentiment in arts and recreation, professional services, and health and education. Meanwhile, firms’ average planned price increases eased to 2.8%, while expected wage growth slipped to 2.2%. Hiring intentions deteriorated further, reaching their weakest level since the pandemic, with sharply more firms planning to lay off (22% vs 16% in September) than hire (11% vs 13%). Weak demand remained the most frequently cited constraint, affecting nearly half of businesses, followed by shortage of skilled labour, while higher fuel, tax, insurance and wage costs continued to pressure operating expenses.

Turkish Stocks Sink — Turkish benchmark BIST 100 index traded below the 11,500 level in late October 2026, hovering near its year-to-date lows, while average daily trading volume fell by around 30% year-on-year over the past month amid turmoil in the investment fund industry. In September, the benchmark stock index plunged around 16%, marking its steepest monthly decline since 2008.

### 2026-10-22 19:31:18  #Trading Economics #Markets #Turkey #Currency #Importance 1

Turkish Lira Hits New Low — The Turkish lira approached 50 per US dollar in October 2026, hitting a fresh record low as authorities maintained a controlled pace of depreciation while intervening in foreign exchange markets to prevent a sharper decline. The lira remains exposed to carry-trade positioning, with investors borrowing in lower-yielding currencies to invest in Turkish assets offering higher returns. At its October meeting, the Central Bank of Turkey cut its benchmark interest rate by 100 basis points to 36%, as expected, marking its first rate cut since the war with Iran began. The decision followed a further easing in inflation, which slowed to 29.73% in September, its lowest level in nearly five years.. The easing cycle is expected to continue into 2027, with the policy rate forecast to fall to 28% by year-end. Meanwhile, the investment fund sector faced a crisis last month after regulators ordered the liquidation of 131 funds, following concerns over asset managers reporting unusually high returns.

### 2026-10-22 19:23:32  #公司 #國際

【阿根廷總統重申將以外交手段收回馬島主權】阿根廷總統米萊21日重申將透過外交途徑收回馬爾維納斯群島（簡稱馬島，英國稱福克蘭群島）主權。 米萊21日在西班牙首都馬德里出席一場活動時說，“不相信戰爭能解決問題”，將透過外交手段收回馬島主權。 米萊將馬島主權爭端與位於阿根廷內烏肯省的巴卡穆埃爾塔油田開採相聯絡，表示將禁止在馬島及爭議海域開採自然資源的外國公司參與該油田業務。 阿根廷政府近來在馬島主權問題上表態強硬。米萊9月3日晚就馬島問題發表全國電視講話時強調，將對在馬島及周邊爭議海域開採自然資源的外國公司加大制裁力度。阿外交部9月22日向英國駐阿大使館發出照會，抗議馬島當局在英方支援下延長相關企業在馬島及爭議海域的油氣開採許可證有效期。 阿根廷和英國對馬島主權存有爭議。1982年4月，阿英兩國因馬島主權歸屬問題爆發戰爭，戰爭以英國勝利而告終。阿根廷戰敗後一直沒有放棄對馬島主權的要求，英國則拒絕就馬島主權問題進行談判。(新華社)

【全球最大主權財富基金挪威主權基金三季度虧損300億美元，債市承壓拖累收益】挪威主權財富基金（全球規模最大主權基金）週四表示，受利率走高壓制債券持倉收益影響，投資組合第三季度虧損300億美元。管理這隻規模2.3萬億美元基金的挪威銀行投資管理局（NBIM）稱，本季度賬面回報虧損2780億克朗（摺合297億美元）。挪威銀行投資管理局固收組合中約70%配置於國債，其餘投向企業債。第三季度全球債券市場承壓。美聯儲等主要央行開啟加息週期，疊加市場對通脹和政府債務規模的擔憂升溫，國債收益率大幅上行。挪威銀行投資管理局固收投資回報率為-3.5%，基金管理層稱這是整體收益下滑的主要原因。

### 2026-10-22 19:10:44  #Trading Economics #Markets #United Kingdom #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

UK Gilt Yields Ease — The UK 10-year gilt yield slipped below 5.4% as concerns over social unrest and rising debt across the eurozone prompted investors to shift away from regional assets, boosting demand for UK government bonds and sterling. Meanwhile, investors continued to weigh stronger-than-expected inflation and mounting public borrowing ahead of next week’s budget and the Bank of England’s November 5 policy meeting. UK inflation rose to 3.5% in September, exceeding forecasts, while core and services inflation also surprised to the upside. Markets now expect at least three quarter-point rate hikes by April 2027, with the first potentially coming in November. Meanwhile, public borrowing reached £24.5 billion, bringing April–September borrowing to £97.8 billion, £6 billion above the OBR’s projection. Despite the worsening fiscal outlook, some major investors expect the upcoming budget to prioritize fiscal discipline, a move that could help sustain demand for gilts.

【Shanghai to push high-quality urban renewal, implement Housing Quality Improvement Project】Shanghai municipal Party committee Financial and Economic Work Committee met this morning to review progress on a new real estate development model. ### 2026-10-22 19:06:21  #Trading Economics #Economy #Turkey #Interest Rate #Importance 2

Turkey Cuts Policy Rate to 36% as Expected — Turkey’s central bank cut its one-week repo rate by 100 basis points to 36% from 37%, lowering its overnight lending and borrowing rates by the same amount to 39% and 34.5%, respectively, as expected. The Monetary Policy Committee said recent inflation data and leading indicators continued to signal a downward trend, while economic activity pointed to persistently weak domestic demand. However, elevated energy prices amid ongoing geopolitical tensions remain a source of upward inflationary pressure. The bank reiterated its commitment to maintaining tight monetary conditions until price stability is achieved, supporting disinflation through demand, exchange rate and expectations channels. Future rate decisions will depend on inflation trends, expectations and progress toward interim targets, with adjustments made cautiously at each meeting. The committee warned that a significant and sustained deterioration in the inflation outlook could prompt monetary tightening.

Gasoline Futures Rebound — US gasoline futures rose to $3.18 per gallon after hitting a seven-week low, as concerns over tight fuel supplies persisted. EIA data showed US gasoline inventories fell by nearly 3 million barrels in the week ending October 16th, reversing previous builds and reinforcing signs of a tightening market. Supplies are expected to remain constrained as refineries prioritise higher-demand diesel ahead of winter, while routine maintenance limits gasoline output despite facilities operating near capacity. Morgan Stanley warned that tight conditions could persist through mid-2027 as planned refinery outages increase. Geopolitical risks added to market concerns, with Russia and Ukraine exchanging heavy air strikes just hours after President Putin agreed to resume peace talks with a US envoy, casting doubt on diplomatic progress. Meanwhile, US-Iran strikes continue in the Middle East, although crude exports through the Strait of Hormuz have recovered towards pre-conflict levels.

Sensex Finishes Lower — India’s BSE Sensex closed about 0.6% down at 73,249 on Thursday, the lowest in over a week, extending losses for the third session. Investor sentiment remained subdued amid concerns that persistently high crude oil prices could widen India’s import bill, fuel inflation and squeeze corporate margins. Meanwhile, RBI policymakers signalled further rate hikes may be needed but cautioned against aggressive tightening amid growth risks. Tempering market's mood, foreign investors turned net buyers and purchased equities worth bought 10.22 billion rupees on Wednesday, while Jio Platforms’ $3.2 billion IPO was fully subscribed within hours. Reliance Industries was the biggest laggard, falling 2.6%, amid profit-booking following a solid advance the day before. Eternal slipped 2.2% as it reported strong revenue growth and a sharp improvement in operating profitability in Q2 FY27, but its net profit fell short of analyst expectations. HDFC Bank (-1.9%) and Power Grid (-1.9%) also posted declines.

【小鵬釋出IRON機器人互動實測影片，展示無語音指令下的手勢響應】近日，小鵬汽車釋出《小鵬機器人擬人實驗室》互動實測影片，展示人形機器人IRON在無語音指令情況下，隨測試者手勢變化調整動作的能力。演示中，測試者連續切換握手、碰拳和擊掌動作，IRON作出相應調整，並在擊掌位置改變後換手接應。據小鵬介紹，IRON每0.2秒根據視覺畫面重新推理一次，並預測未來6秒內的動作變化，透過持續感知與動作調整完成互動。該能力依託小鵬“One Infra, Two Products”物理AI體系，由同一套AI底座支援智慧汽車與人形機器人。

【PHP高危漏洞曝光：360免費開放圖龍鋒安全體檢服務，主動排查漏洞風險】全球廣泛使用的伺服器端程式語言PHP被曝出兩個高危安全漏洞（CVE-2026-109618、CVE-2026-109619），統稱"PHP2Shell"。在特定條件下，攻擊者無需身份認證即可利用相關漏洞實現遠端程式碼執行，可能導致網站被篡改、敏感資訊洩露甚至伺服器失陷。360第一時間完成漏洞復現與攻擊路徑驗證，並給出修復建議。同時，360宣佈面向企業使用者免費開放圖龍鋒安全體檢服務，協助企業排查相關風險。（新浪科技）

【MOFCOM to issue work plan to accelerate consumer adoption of smart products】Oct 22 — At a State Council Information Office briefing on implementing the 15th Five‑Year Plan, the Ministry of Commerce (MOFCOM) said it will issue a work plan to accelerate application of smart products in the consumer market. MOFCOM said it will push full‑chain expansion and upgrading of goods consumption, including measures to expand auto consumption and foster new auto consumption business models and formats. It will also boost service consumption via a service‑quality and public‑benefit campaign and pilots of new consumption models and scenes to cultivate service growth points. Regionally, MOFCOM will deepen development of international consumption centre cities, implement urban commerce quality upgrades and accelerate measures to address county‑level consumption shortfalls.

【商務部：將出臺加快智慧產品在消費領域應用工作方案】10月22日，國新辦舉行介紹推進“十五五”時期商務高質量發展有關情況的新聞釋出會。商務部市場執行和消費促進司司長楊沐介紹了擴內需促消費的話題。她表示，商務部將按照“十五五”規劃部署，順應消費發展新特點新趨勢，強化精準分類施策，供需兩端協同發力，鞏固消費對經濟發展的主引擎作用。一是推動商品消費擴容升級。全鏈條擴大汽車消費，發展汽車消費新業態、新模式。出臺加快智慧產品在消費領域應用工作方案，為新產業新賽道拓展市場空間。二是持續擴大服務消費。深入實施服務消費提質惠民行動，推進消費新業態新模式新場景試點，積極培育服務消費新增長點。三是梯次釋放不同區域消費潛力。深化國際消費中心城市培育建設，實施城市商業提質行動，加快補齊縣域消費短板。（澎湃新聞）

UK Manufacturing Orders Deteriorate Sharply in October — The UK’s total order book balance fell to -32 in October 2026, down from -9 in September and well below market expectations of -20, according to a survey by the Confederation of British Industry (CBI). The reading points to a sharp deterioration in new orders, highlighting continued challenges facing the UK manufacturing sector. CBI Senior Economist Cameron Martin said that persistently high costs were squeezing profitability, while weakening competitiveness and fragile business confidence continued to weigh on investment decisions. However, the survey’s quarterly measure of output expectations improved to -1, suggesting a potential stabilization in manufacturing activity in the months ahead. Meanwhile, the quarterly gauge of domestic price expectations rose to +24 from +11, indicating that manufacturers anticipate stronger price pressures.

【對話豫園珠寶時尚集團CEO韓偉：奢侈品永遠是小眾的，老廟選擇講“好運”】2026可持續全球領導者大會於10月20日至22日在上海黃浦世博園區舉辦。面對業內出現的“奢侈品化““高階定製”新方向，上海豫園珠寶時尚集團有限公司CEO韓偉表示：“奢侈品永遠是小眾的。老廟的好運文化意味著我們絕不能只走小眾路線，我們必須要老少皆宜。”在他看來，珠寶行業正處在消費需求和行業競爭格局的重構期，國潮、輕奢化、定製化三大趨勢疊加，“與其被動跟隨潮流，不如主動去重新定義我們的品牌價值”。 韓偉認為，在這三大趨勢下，消費者的悅己需求明顯增強，老廟的重塑由此展開。他把“老廟給您帶來好運氣”拆成兩個維度的價值表達：一是“越努力越好運”，對應年輕消費者努力、積極、向上的生活觀；二是“越久遠越好運”，以非遺為抓手，向消費者傳達黃金珠寶的文化屬性與孃家豫園的深厚歷史底蘊。

Sterling Holds at One-Month High — The pound held at $1.34, its highest level in more than a month, as rising borrowing costs across the eurozone triggered outflows from regional assets, supporting demand for UK bonds and the currency. Sterling also drew support from stronger-than-expected UK inflation data, with annual inflation rising to 3.5% in September, its highest level since October 2025 and above forecasts of 3.4%. Core and services inflation also exceeded expectations, prompting markets to increase bets on further monetary tightening. Money markets priced in at least three quarter-point rate hikes by April 2027, with the first potentially coming as early as November 5. Meanwhile, concerns over elevated oil prices, the cost of AI infrastructure investment and mounting debt burdens in highly indebted countries such as France weighed on risk appetite. Investors also looked ahead to next week’s budget, where Chancellor John Healey is expected to emphasize fiscal discipline, potentially bolstering demand for gilts.

### 2026-10-22 18:08:14  #其他

【美團外賣薛冰：外賣行業重回積極向好，將幫商家更有含金量地增長】“泡沫逐漸消退、訂單結構更健康，外賣行業重回積極向好的訊號明顯，”10月22日，美團外賣總經理薛冰在2026外賣產業大會上表示，美團將透過各種方式幫助商家實現更有含金量的增長。大會披露資料顯示，今年上半年，全國餐飲外賣商家的營收增速領先餐飲大盤，薛冰表示，這一資料說明外賣需求仍在持續增長。以剛過去的國慶為例，國慶假期期間，外賣消費比平時更加活躍，下沉市場交易額比平日高出26%。薛冰認為，這說明外賣正在從正餐需求延伸至覆蓋全天、隨需隨點的生活服務。同時，今年以來餐飲外賣商家的訂單結構也正在改善。大會發布的資料顯示，2026年餐飲商家外賣訂單中，15元以下低價訂單佔比同比減半，商家外賣訂單的價值正在回升，行業過去的低價泡沫正在消退。“外賣行業迎來了積極向好的訊號，泡沫消退，未來增長會更有含金量。”薛冰表示，“商家過去一直關心在外賣上有沒有賺到錢，在需求增長、訂單結構改善的情況下，美團將繼續和商家一起，把產品、店型、運營和創新一起做好，幫商家實現訂單利潤同向增長，讓商家得到確切的收益和回報”。

### 2026-10-22 17:45:48  #Trading Economics #Markets #Switzerland #Government Bond 10Y #Importance 1

Swiss Bond Yield Retreats to One-Month Low — The yield on the Swiss 10-year government bond fell to 0.5%, its lowest level in over a month, supported by safe-haven demand amid concerns over Europe's fiscal outlook. In contrast, Switzerland has one of the lowest levels of public debt in Europe, with its 2026 budget seen remaining in surplus, aided by a sharp rise in corporate tax revenue that funded a temporary boost to defense spending. Still, Finance Minister Keller-Sutter voiced concerns last week of the risks of rising public debt levels in Europe to economic stability. Adding to the country's stability, SNB meeting minutes showed policymakers saw inflationary pressures with no signs of spillover effects into the economy, attributing the rise in inflation to higher oil prices and the franc's depreciation. The SNB kept its policy rate at 0%, with Chair Schlegel stating no adjustment was currently needed as inflation remains stable. While economists expect rates to hold through 2027, markets are pricing in a hike in early 2027.

### 2026-10-22 17:35:57  #宏觀 #焦點 #國際

【新版《對外投資管理辦法》今天釋出 12月1日起施行】國家發展改革委今天公佈《對外投資管理辦法》，新辦法將進一步促進對外投資高質量發展，保護投資者及其對外投資合法權益，維護國家主權、安全、發展利益。新辦法共76條，分為總則、對外投資指導和服務、對外投資監管、對外投資保護、法律責任、附則等六章。在保持政策延續性的基礎上，新辦法適應新形勢，和此前施行的2017年出臺的版本相比，作了三方面修訂。新辦法將更好契合對外投資發展實際。新辦法規定投資者包括境內企業、其他組織和居民個人，進一步落實各類投資者的對外投資主體地位，完善服務和保護措施，支援投資者按照市場化原則開展對外投資活動。同時，新辦法與現行制度作了銜接，投資者可以繼續透過合格境內機構投資者、港股通、跨境理財通等渠道在境外金融市場投資。新辦法將更好保護對外投資權益。近年來，少數國家和地區採取歧視性措施，有的要求中方提供技術、資料或者處分股權、資產等，損害了中方權益。針對此類問題，新辦法完善了重大不利情況報告制度，設定了對外投資資訊年度報告制度，覆蓋了上述情形。此外，新辦法將更好防範應對境外風險。近年來，一些國家和地區局勢動盪、政策多變，政治、安全等風險上升。有的投資者對境外風險估計不足、應對不力，最終遭受損失。針對此類問題，新辦法完善了境外再投資報告等制度，同時，面向少數金額大、關乎我國與有關國家外交關係的專案設定了前期工作情況報告制度。投資者將上述情況告知管理部門，有助於合力防範應對境外風險。新辦法將於2026年12月1日起施行，屆時2017年出臺的《企業境外投資管理辦法》同步廢止。（央視新聞）

### 2026-10-22 17:34:43  #宏觀 #焦點 #國際

【國家發改委釋出對外投資管理辦法】國家發改委釋出對外投資管理辦法。其中提出，實行備案管理的範圍是投資者直接開展的非敏感類對外投資，也即涉及投資者直接投入資產、權益或者提供融資、擔保等的非敏感類對外投資。投資者是中央管理企業的，備案機關是國家發展改革委；投資者是地方企業，且中方投資額3億美元及以上的，備案機關是國家發展改革委；投資者是地方企業，且中方投資額3億美元以下的，備案機關是投資者註冊地的省級政府發展改革部門；投資者是其他組織的，參照企業執行；投資者是居民個人的，備案機關是投資者戶籍所在地或者經常居住地的省級政府發展改革部門。屬於備案管理範圍內的對外投資，投資者應當在實施對外投資前取得對外投資備案通知書。

### 2026-10-22 17:33:39  #Trading Economics #Economy #Morocco #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Morocco CPI Rebounds in September — Consumer prices in Morocco rose by 0.5% year-on-year in September 2026, recording their strongest increase since May. The rebound followed two consecutive months of deflation in July and August. Upward pressure came primarily from prices of transportation (9.4% vs 7.3% in August), alongside miscellaneous goods & services (3.9% vs 3.2%) and alcoholic beverages & tobacco (3.9% vs 3.9%) and restaurants & hotels (2.9% vs 2.3%). On a monthly basis, consumer prices advanced by 1% in September, the most since March, after rising by 0.8% in the month before. The highest rates were seen for food & non-alcholic beverages (1.7%), transportation (1%) and education (1.2%).

### 2026-10-22 16:44:09  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hong Kong #Inflation Rate #Importance 1

Hong Kong Inflation Rises More Than Expected — Hong Kong’s annual inflation rate rose to 1.9% in September 2026 from 1.7% in August, above market expectations of 1.8%. This marked the highest reading in three months, as price growth accelerated for electricity, gas and water (12.9% vs 11.5% in August), transport (5.2% vs 3.9%), miscellaneous services (5.3% vs 4.7%), and housing (0.2% vs 0.1%). Inflation also picked up for durable goods, reaching 1.1% from 0.8%, while clothing and footwear prices rose 0.3% after remaining unchanged a month earlier. Meanwhile, food inflation eased slightly to 0.5% from 0.6%, and price growth slowed for miscellaneous goods (2.1% vs 2.5%). Prices for alcoholic drinks and tobacco fell 0.4% after remaining unchanged in August. On a monthly basis, consumer prices rose 0.3%, following a 0.1% increase in August. The underlying Composite CPI, which excludes the effects of all one-off government relief measures, rose 2.1% year-on-year, up from 1.9% in August.

Proposed measures include contributions from banks and insurers, although details remain unresolved amid industry opposition, alongside potential household tax relief and increased defence and energy spending.

### 2026-10-22 15:50:24  #Trading Economics #Economy #Philippines #Government Budget Value #Importance 1

Philippines Budget Deficit Widens in September — The Philippines’ budget deficit widened to PHP 248.9 billion in September 2026 from PHP 248.1 billion in the corresponding month of the previous year. Total revenues climbed 12.95% year-on-year to PHP 318.2 billion, driven by increases in both tax collections (12.11%) and non-tax revenues (27.22%). Customs receipts rose 14.76% to PHP 92.1 billion, while cumulative collections benefited from higher VAT and duty receipts amid peso depreciation and elevated oil prices. Total expenditures rose 7.04% to PHP 567.1 billion, led by higher disbursements for education, health and social protection services, as well as infrastructure. Primary expenditures rose 7.46%, accounting for 84.91% of total spending, while interest payments grew 4.74%. In the first nine months of the year, the country recorded a budget shortfall of PHP 1,303.2 billion, up from PHP 1,117.3 billion in the same period last year.

The improvement was driven mainly by overall order books (-11 vs -16 in September) and foreign order books (-1 vs -10), with the latter reaching its highest level since April 2022. Personal production prospects increased for the third consecutive month to 19 from 14, their highest level since February 2022, while past production rebounded to 2 from -1. General production prospects also improved for the sixth consecutive month to -8 from -9, returning to their long-term average. Meanwhile, finished-goods inventories declined to 9 from 13, while expected workforce size edged up to -1 from -2. Expectations for selling prices eased to 11 from 15 but remained above their long-term average. Economic uncertainty among business leaders rose to 36 from 32.

### 2026-10-22 14:56:52  #Trading Economics #Markets #Commodity #Importance 1

Iron Ore Hits Fresh 4-Year Low — Iron ore futures declined toward CNY 670 per ton, reaching their lowest level in four years after China’s state-backed iron ore buyer secured a new supply agreement with Rio Tinto, easing concerns over supply availability. The China Mineral Resources Group verbally informed several steel mills of the deal on Wednesday, although specific terms were not disclosed. CMRG had previously instructed some steel mills to suspend negotiations with Rio Tinto over shipments scheduled from September. Meanwhile, rising global shipments and increased arrivals at Chinese ports signaled abundant supply. Industry data showed that global iron ore shipments rose by 3.89 million tons week-on-week to 34.77 million tons in the week ending October 18. Shipments from Australia and Brazil increased by a combined 4.79 million tons to 29.82 million tons, while iron ore arrivals at 47 Chinese ports climbed by 2.73 million tons to 28.35 million tons over the same period.

### 2026-10-22 14:52:22  #Trading Economics #Economy #Hungary #Unemployment Rate #Importance 1

Hungary Jobless Rate Rises to 4.7% in July–September — The unemployment rate in Hungary rose to 4.7% in the July–September 2026 period, up from 4.5% in the corresponding period a year earlier. The number of unemployed individuals increased by 8.4 thousand from a year ago to 229.9 thousand. Of the total unemployed, among men, the unemployment rate stood at 4.5%, with 115.8 thousand unemployed, while the number of unemployed women remained higher at 114.1 thousand, with an unemployment rate of 4.9%. Moreover, the average time spent looking for a job was 11.6 months. Among unemployed individuals, 43.1% had been seeking work for less than three months, while the share looking for work for more than a year increased by 3.3 percentage points to 32.5%. Meanwhile, the number of employed people decreased to 10 thousand year-on-year to 4.669 million. Employment among men reached 2.475 million, while employment among women decreased by 15.0 thousand to 2.194 million.

### 2026-10-22 14:42:30  #Trading Economics #Markets #Mexico #Currency #Importance 1

Mexican Peso Hits 1-Year Low — The Mexican peso weakened to around 18.78 per US dollar in October, reaching its weakest level in nearly a year as a narrowing interest-rate differential with the US, geopolitical uncertainty and stalled USMCA negotiations weighed on sentiment. Expectations of further easing by the Bank of Mexico, alongside the Federal Reserve's September rate hike, have reduced the peso's yield advantage and encouraged investors to unwind carry trades. Meanwhile, uncertainty surrounding the Middle East conflict has dampened risk appetite, while limited progress in the US-Mexico-Canada Agreement review has clouded the trade outlook. Investors are now awaiting Mexico's first-half October inflation data, with headline inflation expected to accelerate for a third consecutive period to 3.60% year-on-year from 3.49%, potentially reinforcing expectations that Banxico will keep interest rates unchanged for an extended period.

【報告：東南亞AI滲透率走高，生成式AI使用者達2.43億】谷歌、淡馬錫、貝恩聯合釋出《東南亞數字經濟報告》顯示，不到三年東南亞生成式AI使用者達2.43億，覆蓋當地58%勞動人口，普及速度是移動網際網路的4倍，但僅1%-2%使用者願意付費訂閱個人AI服務，AI商業化更多依靠企業端落地變現。東南亞消費者對AI接受度高，調查顯示75%的消費者願意交由AI代理比價下單，62%的消費者對AI工具的信任度較去年提升。不過人均AI使用強度僅為美國的10%。同樣AI在企業端的收益也已經顯現：報告顯示，AI可拉高電商轉化率、客單價，Shopee藉助AI客服承接80%諮詢，單客對接成本下降30%，AI廣告投放也顯著帶動點選率與轉化。報告預測，今年東南亞數字經濟GMV同比增17%至3680億美元，2035年有望突破1萬億美元。線上旅遊、數字媒體、網約車外賣等賽道也將保持穩健增長。

Copper Prices Slide to 5-Week Low — Copper futures fell toward $6.40 per pound on Thursday, reaching their lowest level in five weeks as a global selloff in artificial intelligence stocks dampened sentiment toward the metal. Copper remains a key beneficiary of AI expansion due to its essential role in data centers and power infrastructure. Prices also faced pressure this week amid speculation that the US may abandon proposed tariffs on refined copper imports. Markets increasingly expect the Trump administration to reconsider the measures ahead of the midterm elections, as global supplies tighten and inflationary pressures persist. Copper prices hit record highs last month after traders redirected shipments to US warehouses in anticipation of potential tariffs, reducing availability in other major markets. Meanwhile, supply concerns remain elevated amid the risk of labor strikes at major mining companies due to ongoing labor disputes.

### 2026-10-22 14:31:02  #Trading Economics #Economy #Norway #Unemployment Rate #Importance 2

Norway Jobless Rate Rises to 13-Month High — Norway's seasonally adjusted unemployment rate rose to 5.0% in September 2026 from 4.6% in the previous month, marking the highest level since June 2025. The number of unemployed persons increased to a fifteen-month high of 156 thousand from 141 thousand in August. The youth unemployment rate, which covers those aged 15 to 24, climbed to 16.7% from 14.2%. Meanwhile, the employment rate remained unchanged at 69.8% in September, while the number of employed persons edged higher to 2.930 million from 2.925 million. The labor force participation rate rose to 73.5% from 73.1%, with the labor force increasing by 21 thousand from a month earlier to 3.086 million. Total man-weeks worked, adjusted to a 37.5-hour workweek, decreased to 2.187 million from 2.239 million in the previous period. Compared with September last year, the jobless rate was 0.3 percentage points lower at 4.7%.

Taiwan Stocks End at 5-Week Low — The TAIEX, Taiwan’s benchmark stock index, fell 846 points, or 1.8%, to 46,041 on Thursday, erasing gains from the previous session and tracking declines in US stock futures as investors digested earnings from Meta and Tesla. Rising oil prices further pressured sentiment, raising concerns over inflation expectations and interest rate hikes amid escalating tensions in the Middle East and the Russia-Ukraine conflict. The broader index fell to its lowest level since September 17, weighed mainly by electronic technology, producer manufacturing, process industries, and commercial services. Electronic technology stocks shed 2.3% amid ongoing concerns over AI investment, with TSMC, the world’s largest contract chipmaker, which accounts for more than 40% of the market’s total value, falling 1.5%. Meanwhile, MediaTek, Delta Electronics, and Hon Hai Precision fell 3.1%, 4.0%, and 2.0%, respectively. Process industries plunged 3.8%, mainly due to a 6.1% slump in Nan Ya Plastics Corporation.

【IMF：受伊朗衝突影響，中東北非及巴基斯坦地區今年經濟產出料將萎縮1.3%】國際貨幣基金組織（IMF）週四預測，受伊朗衝突影響，中東、北非及巴基斯坦地區的經濟產出今年將大幅下滑；預計到2027年，該地區經濟將出現反彈，實現6.6%的增長。同時，該組織警告稱，如果能源生產進一步受阻，經濟前景可能會進一步惡化。IMF表示，由於衝突對能源生產、航運、旅遊、貿易及市場信心的衝擊，預計2026年該地區的經濟產出將收縮1.3%。儘管2027年經濟產出有望大幅增長，但這些經濟體的整體產出水平仍將低於戰前的預測值。IMF表示，其基準假設是衝突將於2027年初緩解，貿易活動也將在該年內逐步恢復正常；但同時指出，由於存在“極高的不確定性”，前景依然不明朗。

Norway Industrial Confidence Hits Highest Since Q1 2025 — Norway’s industrial confidence rose to 3.4 in Q3 2026 from a downwardly revised 2.5 in the previous quarter, exceeding its long-term average of 2.9. This marked the highest reading since Q1 2025, as total industrial production improved slightly, driven largely by a strong increase in output among intermediate goods manufacturers, particularly non-ferrous metals producers. Consumer goods production also rose moderately, while investment goods output remained unchanged as declining oil and gas investment continued to weigh on the sector. Meanwhile, industrial employment remained broadly flat for a fourth consecutive quarter. On the price front, input costs increased at a faster pace than selling prices. Looking ahead, firms remain optimistic, expecting higher production, employment, order intake, and order backlogs in the fourth quarter.

### 2026-10-22 14:16:36  #Trading Economics #Economy #Denmark #Consumer Confidence #Importance 1

Danish Consumer Morale Falls to 5-Month Low — Denmark’s consumer confidence fell to -17.2 in October 2026 from -13.7 in the previous month, dropping below the six-month average of -15.7 and marking the lowest reading since May, as all categories deteriorated. Assessments of consumers’ financial situation worsened both over the past 12 months (-11.1 vs -8.6 in September) and over the next year (-5.9 vs -3.7). Perceptions of the country’s economic situation also deteriorated, both for the current period (-27.8 vs -22.5) and the year ahead (-26.1 vs -19.2). Consumers’ willingness to make major purchases became more pessimistic (-15.2 vs -14.3). Among other indicators, assessments of current price growth increased (34 vs 28), while inflation expectations for the next 12 months rose (33.5 vs 31.9). Concerns about unemployment over the next year also increased (22 vs 19.2).

### 2026-10-22 14:16:05  #公司

【工信部NVDB釋出關於防範PHP遠端程式碼執行超危漏洞的風險提示】工業和資訊化部網路安全威脅和漏洞資訊共享平臺（NVDB）監測發現，PHP存在兩個遠端程式碼執行超危漏洞，危害嚴重。PHP是一款廣泛用於Web開發的開源伺服器端指令碼語言。由於其存在兩個超危遠端程式碼執行漏洞，未經身份驗證的攻擊者可利用其遠端執行任意程式碼，造成伺服器受控、敏感資訊洩露、業務中斷等危害。受影響的PHP範圍包括：8.5系列＜8.5.12、8.4系列＜8.4.27、8.3系列＜8.3.36、8.2系列＜8.2.35、8.1系列及更早版本。目前，PHP安全團隊已修復漏洞併發布安全公告（URL連結：https://www.php.net/archive/2026.php#2026-10-21-5）。建議相關單位和使用者立即開展全面排查，及時升級至最新安全版本，或採取臨時下線、關閉會話上傳進度功能、網路隔離、網路訪問限制等臨時防護措施；加強網路安全監測和管理，及時修改賬號、口令和金鑰等憑證，防範網路攻擊風險。（工信部）

NZX 50 Halts Gains, Ends Slightly Lower — The NZX 50 dropped 14 points, or 0.1%, to close at 13,747 on Thursday, halting gains from the morning and the previous session as traders assessed Q3 inflation data. Although annual inflation eased to 3.7% in Q3 from a two-year high in Q2, caution persisted amid expectations that inflation would rise again following a temporary dip in fuel prices. Inflation also remained above the RBNZ's 1–3% target range. Rising oil prices further pressured sentiment, raising concerns over inflation expectations and interest rate hikes amid escalating tensions in the Middle East and the Russia-Ukraine conflict. The broader index tracked declines in US stock futures as investors digested earnings from Meta and Tesla. Healthcare, financials, real estate, and consumer discretionary stocks weighed on the index, with the biggest laggards including Hallenstein Glasson (-3.9%), Mainfreight (-3.0%), Fisher & Paykel (-1.1%), Henderson Far East Income (-1.0%), Infratil (-1.0%), and Port of Tauranga (-0.8%).

【中國駐沙特大使館建議中國公民暫勿乘飛機在沙特旅行】據中國駐沙特大使館訊息，10月22日凌晨，沙特民航總局釋出公告稱：達曼法赫德國王國際機場於10月21日（星期三）晚間遭襲，目前已造成1死8傷，物質損失尚在評估中。另有官方訊息稱，一架亞丁航空客機在本次襲擊中受損，機上1人受傷。連日來，沙特利雅得、達曼等地機場和重要設施持續遭導彈和無人機襲擊。利雅得哈立德國王國際機場和一些南部城市機場目前仍無恢復運營確切資訊，其他城市機場航班經常出現大面積異常。中國駐沙特使領館再次提醒在沙中資機構和中國公民務必提高安全防範意識，減少非必要外出，遠離機場、石油設施、軍事基地等敏感地區，勿拍攝軍事行動和敏感設施。建議近期勿乘飛機在沙特旅行，已購票旅客請與有關航司保持密切聯絡，關注航班變化和有關機場情況。發生爭議時保留好相關證據，理性維權。擬陸路從第三國出入沙特人員請注意有關國家入境規定，攜帶好重要證件。長途行車務必注意交通安全，遵守交通規則。

【WMO: Current El Niño could reach unprecedented intensity】The World Meteorological Organization on the 21st released its Global Seasonal Climate Update, saying the current El Niño could reach an unprecedented intensity. It forecasts tropical Pacific sea surface temperatures may be about 3.8°C above average in Nov 2026–Jan 2027. WMO secretary-general Shao Luo said that projection is outside the range of charts monitored over the past 40 years. El Niño is expected to peak late this year, though impacts could persist beyond the peak.

Dutch Consumer Confidence Falls Slightly in October — Consumer confidence in the Netherlands edged down to -34 in October 2026 from September’s six-month high of -33. The economic climate sub-indicator declined to -59 from -58 in the previous month, as consumers were more pessimistic about the economy over the past 12 months (-67 vs -65), while their assessment of the economy for the next 12 months remained unchanged at -50. Willingness to buy also weakened slightly to -18 from -17, reflecting a more negative assessment of households’ financial situations, both over the past 12 months (-19 vs -16) and the coming 12 months (-2 vs 0). However, consumers viewed the timing of major purchases, such as furniture and electronics, slightly more favorably in October, with the indicator improving to -34 from -35 in September.

【Ministry of Water Resources activates Level IV flood‑defense response for Shanghai, Jiangsu, Zhejiang, Fujian】The Ministry of Water Resources activated a Level IV flood‑defense emergency response at 12:00 on Oct. 22 for Shanghai, Jiangsu, Zhejiang and Fujian ahead of Typhoon Qiangpi. Qiangpi is forecast to bring a strong rain episode Oct. 23–25 to eastern Jiangnan and eastern Jianghuai, mainly moderate to heavy rain, with parts of Shanghai and eastern Zhejiang liable to heavy to very heavy rain. Coastal and basin water levels are expected to rise in Fujian’s Jiaoxi, Zhejiang’s Feiyun, Oujiang, Jiaojiang, Yaojiang and the Cao’e (a tributary of the Qiantang), Jiangsu’s Lixiahe region, and Taihu and surrounding river networks; some small rivers in Zhejiang’s coastal heavy‑rain areas may exceed warning levels. Hill and mountainous areas face elevated flash‑flood risk. The ministry directed local water authorities and basin managers to intensify monitoring, forecasting and warnings, maintain emergency duty and joint assessment, scientifically operate hydraulic projects, secure reservoirs and works under construction, and strengthen defenses for small‑and‑medium rivers and mountain torrents.

Rupiah Extends Losses as Dollar Strength, Fiscal Risks Weigh — The Indonesian rupiah weakened toward IDR 18,040 per U.S. dollar on Thursday, continuing its previous decline amid broad-based dollar strength ahead of next week’s Federal Reserve meeting, where interest rates are widely expected to remain unchanged. Locally, oil prices around USD 100 per barrel added to fiscal concerns, while a steeper rise in food costs linked to El Niño sustained inflationary pressures. Sentiment was further dampened by President Prabowo’s criticism of MSCI’s proposed changes to Indonesia’s market classification, deepening worries about potential foreign capital outflows. Still, Bank Indonesia’s liquidity measures may have helped cushion the decline. The central bank held its key rate steady for a fourth straight month in October but raised the deposit facility rate by 25bps to 5%. It also announced reserve requirement cuts for commercial banks, from 9% to 8% from Nov. 1 and to 7% starting Dec. 1, expected to inject around IDR 190 trillion into the banking system.

【特斯拉歐洲月度銷量激增】特斯拉上月歐洲銷量激增，顯示埃隆·馬斯克旗下這家電動汽車製造商在歐洲繼續擴大市場影響力；中國競爭對手在這一地區也在強勁增長。行業機構歐洲汽車製造商協會(European Automobile Manufacturers' Association，簡稱ACEA)的資料顯示，作為銷量指標的特斯拉車型新車註冊量9月份同比躍升72.5%，至68,776輛，統計範圍涵蓋歐盟(European Union)、英國、冰島、列支敦斯登、挪威和瑞士。上月增速較8月份僅4%的增幅明顯加快；8月份通常是汽車註冊淡季，因為歐洲許多經銷商會因夏季假期休業。得益於相對低價的電動和混合動力車型陣容，中國汽車製造商近年來在歐洲迅速崛起。ACEA稱，比亞迪(BYD)上月註冊量激增近99%，至49,953輛。上汽集團(SAIC Motor)註冊量增長近24%，至41,987輛；零跑汽車(Leapmotor)註冊量增至14,594輛，達到原來的四倍多。相比之下，德國大眾汽車銷量下降2.9%，至308,474輛；雷諾銷量下降0.7%，至114,726輛。

### 2026-10-22 12:01:35  #Trading Economics #Economy #European Union #Car Registrations #Importance 2

EU Passenger Car Registrations Hit 3-Month High — EU passenger car registrations grew 9.3% yoy to a three-month high of 971,048 units in September 2026, marking the eighth straight month of growth and accelerating from a 4.5% rise in August. The market continued to benefit from robust consumer demand for a range of electrified vehicles, driven primarily by market support measures and a broader model offering. The bloc’s four largest markets all registered notable growth: France (11.6%), Spain (10.2%), Italy (9.8%), and Germany (9.0%). In the 1st nine months of 2026, new EU car registrations rose 5.7%, despite a challenging macroeconomic environment, persistent geopolitical headwinds, and rising energy prices. Battery-electric vehicles (BEVs) accounted for 22.7% of the EU market, highlighting continued momentum in the shift toward electrification. The three largest markets, which together accounted for 64% of total BEV registrations, posted strong growth: France (79.1%), Germany (58.1%), and Denmark (40.3%).

【聯合國發出警告：必須大幅削減排放 以限制全球變暖幅度超出預期目標】聯合國在將於土耳其舉辦的第 31 屆聯合國氣候變化大會（COP31）前夕表示，全球已無法將升溫控制在 1.5 攝氏度以內，當下必須全力縮小升溫超標的幅度。而美國退出氣候行動陣營，令這項任務難上加難。聯合國環境規劃署（UNEP）於週三釋出年度《排放差距報告》稱，若要實現 “儘可能小幅度超標” 的目標，各國需在 2030 年前，在 2025 年排放水平基礎上，削減近五分之一的二氧化碳排放量。聯合國環境規劃署設定的這一情景是：將升溫控制在較工業化前水平高 1.7 至 1.8 攝氏度，並在本世紀末讓全球平均升溫回落至 1.5 攝氏度。報告稱，所需的減排行動要做到“即刻、深度、快速且持續”。報告顯示，二十國集團（G20）經濟體中，僅有不到一半有望兌現各自現有的 2030 年目標；按照當前政策推演，到 2100 年全球升溫將達到 2.6 攝氏度。

Palm Oil Edges Higher as Export Demand Offsets Ample Supply — Malaysian palm oil futures inched up, hovering above MYR 4,750 per tonne after recent losses, supported by robust exports and stronger demand prospects. Cargo surveyors reported a 58.5%–78.4% surge in shipments during October 1–20 from the same period in September, while festival-related demand from India, a key importer, further bolstered sentiment. Higher crude oil prices also lent support by enhancing palm oil’s competitiveness as a biodiesel feedstock. Meanwhile, Malaysia is considering a temporary waiver or reduction of its 10% crude palm oil export tax to help clear record-high inventories and improve global price competitiveness. Stocks reached an all-time high of 3.51 million tonnes in September. However, gains were capped by weaker palm olein futures in Dalian and lower soyoil prices in Chicago. Seasonal production is also expected to remain elevated in October following six consecutive monthly increases, reinforcing concerns over ample supplies.

【南非下調經濟增長預期 臨時削減燃油稅】南非財政部長埃諾赫·戈東瓜納21日在南非立法首都開普敦宣佈，將2026年南非經濟增長預期下調至1.2%，同時臨時削減燃油稅以緩解國際油價上漲帶來的壓力。 戈東瓜納在議會發表中期預算政策宣告時說，中東衝突若持續，加之厄爾尼諾現象及其對生產的潛在影響，南非經濟增長可能進一步承壓。南非2026年經濟增長預期由今年2月預測的1.6%下調至1.2%；預計2027年經濟增長回升到1.6%，2029年增長達到2%左右。 戈東瓜納說，為緩解民眾生活和企業生產成本壓力，南非政府將於11月4日至2027年1月5日，分階段臨時下調柴油和汽油燃油稅。11月4日至12月1日柴油燃油稅每升下調3蘭特（1美元約合16蘭特），汽油燃油稅每升下調1.5蘭特；此後，減稅幅度將逐步縮小，一直持續至2027年1月5日。 他還表示，儘管經濟增長前景趨弱，但南非的公共財政狀況有所改善。南非政府將繼續推進結構性改革，提升國家治理能力，並加大有利於經濟增長的基礎設施投資，以促進更快、更具包容性的經濟增長。(新華社)

【PHP現嚴重安全漏洞波及全球】當地時間10月21日，據《華爾街日報》報道，全球廣泛使用的程式語言PHP披露嚴重安全漏洞，可能使駭客控制基於PHP執行的伺服器，竊取資訊或對訪問相關網站的使用者發動網路攻擊。該漏洞被命名為“PHP2Shell”，由網路安全研究人員藉助AI工具發現。 PHP 是支撐全球大量網站和 Web 應用的開源指令碼語言，估計有約 70% 的網站依賴其執行。今年8月，網路安全公司Calif的研究人員藉助OpenAI的人工智慧模型GPT-5.6 Sol，在審查PHP原始碼時發現這一漏洞。PHP官方隨後確認，修復嚴重漏洞的新版本已經上線，並“強烈建議”使用者立即升級。（CCTV國際時訊）

### 2026-10-22 11:00:50  #公司

【中移網際網路有限公司原黨委書記洪小勤被開除黨籍】中央紀委國家監委網站10月22日通報，據中央紀委國家監委駐中國移動紀檢監察組、內蒙古自治區紀委監委訊息：日前，經中央紀委國家監委批准，中央紀委國家監委駐中國移動紀檢監察組、內蒙古自治區鄂爾多斯市監察委員會對中移網際網路有限公司原黨委書記、董事長、總經理洪小勤嚴重違紀違法問題進行了立案審查調查。經查，洪小勤理想信念缺失，背棄初心使命，對抗組織審查；無視中央八項規定精神，多次違規收受禮品、禮金、消費卡；違背組織原則，不按規定報告個人有關事項，在職工錄用、職級晉升工作中為他人謀取利益；廉潔底線失守，違規從事營利活動，違規與供應商發生直接經濟往來；利用職務便利，為他人在專案承攬、職工錄用、職務晉升等方面謀利，並非法收受鉅額財物。洪小勤嚴重違反黨的政治紀律、組織紀律、廉潔紀律和生活紀律，構成嚴重職務違法並涉嫌受賄犯罪，且在黨的十八大後不收斂、不收手，性質嚴重，影響惡劣，應予嚴肅處理。依據《中國共產黨紀律處分條例》《中華人民共和國監察法》《中華人民共和國公職人員政務處分法》等有關規定，經中國移動黨組研究，決定給予洪小勤開除黨籍處分；經中央紀委國家監委駐中國移動紀檢監察組研究，決定按規定取消其享受的待遇；收繳其違紀違法所得。內蒙古自治區鄂爾多斯市監察委員會將其涉嫌犯罪問題移送檢察機關依法審查起訴，所涉財物一併移送。

### 2026-10-22 10:59:51  #Trading Economics #Markets #Japan #Currency #Importance 1

Yen Edges Up Ahead of BOJ Policy Meeting — The Japanese yen strengthened slightly beyond 158 per dollar on Thursday but remained within a tight trading range as investors awaited next week’s Bank of Japan policy meeting. The central bank is widely expected to keep interest rates unchanged before potentially raising them in December amid persistent inflationary pressures. On Wednesday, BOJ Deputy Governor Ryozo Himino noted that underlying inflation was approaching the bank’s 2% target and cautioned against delaying policy adjustments, reinforcing hawkish remarks made by Governor Kazuo Ueda earlier this week. However, the yen continued to face pressure from carry trades, as Japan’s relatively low interest rates reduced the currency’s attractiveness. The BOJ’s gradual pace of monetary tightening is also seen as insufficient to significantly narrow the interest rate differential between Japan and other major economies.

### 2026-10-22 10:52:56  #Trading Economics #Markets #Indonesia #Stock Market #Importance 1

BI Support Lifts Indonesian Equities for 3rd Session — Indonesian stocks rose 40 points, or 0.7%, to 5,367 on Thursday morning deals, up for the third straight session as most sectors advanced. Sentiment was buoyed by Bank Indonesia’s latest move to hold its key rate at 5.75% for a fourth month while raising its deposit facility rate by 25bps to support the rupiah. The central bank also gradually trims RRR from Nov. 1, aiming to free up around IDR 190 trillion into the banking system. However, gains were capped after President Prabowo criticised MSCI for proposing to remove Indonesia from its emerging-market status and reclassify it as a standalone market, saying Jakarta would not be intimidated by the plan. Meanwhile, U.S. equity futures weakened amid persistent concerns over returns from heavy tech spending. Non-cyclicals, energy, healthcare, and financials drove the strength, with Merdeka Battery Materials (4.0%), Aneka Tambang (3.3%), and Telkom Indonesia (1.9%) among early standouts. The four big banks rallied between 1.8% and 2.5%.
